Method: 提出MGN-AIR框架：首先学习估计像素级视觉提示，然后结合文本提示和视觉提示为模型提供全局和局部退化线索，指导模型在每个像素位置上如何恢复。

Result: 在多个全合一图像恢复基准上进行了广泛实验，涵盖去噪、去雨、去模糊、去雾、去雪和低光增强等任务，实验结果表明该方法一致且显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 像素级恢复策略比区域级统一策略更能实现精细且准确的图像恢复，MGN-AIR通过像素级视觉提示和多模态线索引导，在多个退化任务上取得了最优性能。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration is a unified low-level vision task that aims to effectively recover high-quality images from inputs degraded by various types and levels of corruption using a single model. Recent works have achieved remarkable progress by learning degradation-adaptive prompts or network architectures. However, these methods typically apply a uniform restoration strategy across the entire image, neglecting the fact that different regions may suffer from distinct degradation types and varying degrees of severity. In contrast, we propose to perform restoration at the pixel level, thereby enabling more fine-grained and precise control over the restoration process. Specifically, we present MGN-AIR, a novel pixel-level restoration framework for all-in-one image restoration. Our approach first learns to estimate a pixel-level visual prompt. Then, it leverages both textual and visual prompts to provide global and local degradation cues, guiding the model on where to look and how to restore at each pixel. We conduct extensive experiments on multiple all-in-one image restoration benchmarks, covering a wide range of tasks including denoising, deraining, deblurring, dehazing, desnowing, and low-light enhancement. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed method consistently and significantly outperforms existing approaches.

</details>


### [221] [GeoRoute: Geometry-Aware Hybrid Inference for Traffic Future-Frame Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09493)
*Khang Minh Le,Hieu Dinh Trung Pham,Luu Thanh Danh,Nam-Tien Le,Hieu Anh Ngo,Phuong Huu Vu Tran,Son Nguyen Minh Le,Nguyen Trong Nghia,Tu Tran Thi Cam,Huy Minh Nhat Nguyen,Cuong Tuan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的推理框架，通过多帧深度分层渲染器稳固静态几何结构，并利用视觉语言模型进行视角条件路由选择专用运动预测器，从而在长时程交通场景未来帧预测中解决时间重影、几何漂移和物体运动不一致问题，在AI City Challenge Track 5上取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程未来帧预测在自动驾驶、交通监控等领域至关重要，但现有潜在视频扩散模型直接应用于结构化交通场景时存在几何不稳定和时间一致性退化的问题。需要一个无需重训练即可提升预测稳定性的推理时解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个训练自由的推理框架：(1) 对前视摄像头视频，使用多帧深度分层渲染器，从历史观测帧投影静态几何并保留生成模型的动态区域；(2) 对异构交通视角，使用冻结的视觉语言模型推断粗粒度相机类型并选择专用的基于运动的预测器。整个框架无需重新训练或微调底层视频生成模型。

Result: 在AI City Challenge Track 5基准上验证了所提框架，最终系统在顶级团队中取得具有竞争力的性能，证明了推理时几何感知细化和视角条件混合推理能提升静态几何稳定性和低级结构保真度。

Conclusion: 几何感知的推理时细化和视角条件混合推理可以在不改变原始模型架构的情况下，有效提升长时程交通场景未来帧预测的静态几何稳定性和结构保真度。

Abstract: Long-horizon future-frame prediction is important for autonomous driving, traffic surveillance, and intelligent transportation systems, yet remains challenging due to temporal ghosting, geometry drift, and inconsistent object motion. Recent latent video diffusion models have achieved impressive visual quality, but directly applying them to structured traffic scenes often leads to unstable geometry and degraded temporal coherence over extended horizons. We present a training-free inference framework that stabilizes reliable static structure in pretrained video predictions through multi-frame temporal context and view-conditioned routing. For front-camera videos, our method refines generated futures with a multi-frame depth-layered renderer that projects static geometry from observed history frames while preserving dynamic regions from the generative base model. For heterogeneous traffic views, a frozen vision-language model infers a coarse camera group from the observed clip and selects a specialized motion-based predictor. The framework requires neither retraining nor fine-tuning of the underlying video model and can be applied directly to pretrained generators. We validate the proposed framework on the AI City Challenge Track 5 benchmark, where our final system achieves competitive performance among the top-ranked teams. These results demonstrate that geometry-aware inference-time refinement and view-conditioned hybrid inference can improve static-geometry stability and low-level structural fidelity without changing the original model architecture.

</details>


### [222] [SwissCrop25: A National Multi-Year Benchmark for Operational Crop Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09497)
*Thomas Lauber,Mehmet Ozgur Turkoglu,Sélène Ledain,Helge Aasen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SwissCrop25，一个覆盖瑞士全国、跨越2019-2025年七个生长季的作物制图基准数据集，包含73个细粒度作物类别、5个非作物土地覆盖类别和温度观测数据，并通过留一年验证协议评估了三种代表性模型架构在现实部署条件下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的作物制图数据集只能孤立地评估跨年份泛化、细粒度作物分类和耕地与周围景观区分这三个关键需求，缺乏能够在真实部署条件下同时评估这三项能力的基准，因此需要构建一个综合性基准数据集。

Method: 构建了整合Sentinel-2时间序列、每日温度观测、73个细粒度作物类别（含草地管理类型）和5个非作物土地覆盖类别的全国尺度基准数据集；定义了留一年验证协议用于联合耕地划定和作物分类，并在季节内进行了时序评估；对比评估了U-TAE（卷积时序注意力模型）、TSViT（基于Transformer的时空模型）和Galileo（地球观测基础模型）。

Result: 领域专用模型（TSViT和U-TAE）优于基础模型Galileo；TSViT取得最佳整体性能，比U-TAE的宏mIoU高出12个百分点；数据集揭示了显著的跨年分布漂移，且温度衍生的物候信息能提升模型鲁棒性；季节内评估显示U-TAE在季初表现更好，而TSViT在季末通过更好的稀有类别区分能力获得优势。

Conclusion: SwissCrop25为在现实操作条件下评估作物制图系统提供了一个具有挑战性的测试平台，揭示了不同架构在传统基准中无法显现的差异，并公开在Hugging Face供研究使用。

Abstract: Operational crop mapping requires models that generalise across years, resolve fine-grained crop taxonomies, and distinguish cropland from surrounding landscapes. However, existing crop mapping datasets enable evaluation of these requirements only in isolation. We therefore introduce SwissCrop25, a national-scale crop mapping benchmark dataset spanning seven growing seasons (2019-2025). SwissCrop25 combines Sentinel-2 time series, daily temperature observations, a fine-grained 73 crop taxonomy including grassland management types, and 5 explicit non-crop land cover classes. To evaluate realistic deployment conditions, we define a leave-one-year-out protocol with joint cropland delineation and crop classification for benchmarking representative crop mapping architectures. Evaluating U-TAE (convolutional temporal-attention model), TSViT (transformer-based spatio-temporal model), and Galileo (EO foundation model) reveals differences between architectures hidden by conventional benchmarks. In this setting, domain-specific models outperform Galileo, with TSViT achieving the best overall performance and a 12 pp macro-mIoU advantage over U-TAE. SwissCrop25 also exposes substantial interannual distribution shifts and shows that incorporating temperature-derived phenological information improves robustness. Finally, in-season evaluation reveals a trade-off between models, with U-TAE performing better early in the season and TSViT gaining an advantage later through improved rare-class discrimination. SwissCrop25 provides a challenging testbed for evaluating crop mapping systems under realistic operational conditions and is publicly released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/EOA-team/SwissCrop25 .

</details>


### [223] [XFeat Revisited: Reproducibility and Evaluation of a Lightweight Image Matcher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09519)
*Lazar Đoković,Aimee Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对轻量级局部特征提取与匹配器XFeat进行了可复现性研究，重新实现了其架构，复现了作者发布的检查点，并通过消融实验和扩展实验（包括同分布基准、跨模态和零样本场景）系统验证了XFeat的准确率-效率权衡主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XFeat的论文、补充材料和公开代码在实现细节上存在差异（包括骨干网络布局、融合块和训练损失），这些不一致使得原始论文中的部分设计选择缺乏充分论证，需要系统性复现研究来厘清其真实性能与设计合理性。

Method: 研究分为三部分：1) 基于论文和补充材料重新实现XFeat架构；2) 在MegaDepth-1500和ScanNet-1500上评估作者的检查点与重新实现的模型，并进行架构消融实验；3) 复现下游任务（单应性估计、Aachen视觉定位），并扩展到零样本跨模态匹配（视网膜、热红外-可见光、多模态遥感图像）。

Result: 重新实现的模型在MegaDepth-1500和ScanNet-1500上与原始检查点性能接近甚至部分超越；并行关键点分支对半稠密匹配重要但收益不如原始论文声称的显著，单跳跃连接的位置证据不充分；单应性估计结果吻合，但Aachen视觉定位低于报告结果；在严重模态偏移下XFeat性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: XFeat的核心主张（强准确率-效率权衡）得到验证，但原始论文中部分架构论据缺乏充分支撑，且其在下游定位任务和极端跨模态场景下的鲁棒性有待提升，提示需要更明确的规范来确保可复现性。

Abstract: We present a reproducibility study of XFeat, a lightweight local feature extractor and matcher designed to identify corresponding points across images efficiently on resource-constrained hardware. We re-implement the architecture based on the paper and supplementary material, re-evaluate the authors' released checkpoint alongside our re-implementation, and conduct additional architectural ablations to examine design choices that were not fully justified in the original work. This distinction between re-evaluation and reproduction is important, as the paper, supplement, and public code differ in several implementation details, including the backbone layout, fusion block, and training losses. Empirically, our reproduced models closely match and, in some cases, outperform the re-evaluated original checkpoint on MegaDepth-1500 and ScanNet-1500, supporting the main claim that XFeat provides a strong accuracy-efficiency trade-off for standard image-matching benchmarks. Our ablations provide a more nuanced view of two architectural arguments from the original paper. In particular, the parallel keypoint branch is important for semi-dense matching, but its benefit is less pronounced than originally claimed, while the evidence for the specific placement of the single skip-connection remains inconclusive. Finally, we reproduce the original downstream evaluations and find close agreement for homography estimation, while Aachen visual localization remains below the reported results, even for the released checkpoint, suggesting sensitivity to underspecified evaluation details. We then extend the analysis to zero-shot out-of-distribution and cross-modal matching across retinal, thermal-visible, and multimodal remote-sensing imagery, where XFeat remains effective in some settings but degrades sharply under severe modality shifts.

</details>


### [224] [A Height-Constrained 2-Point Minimal Solver for Pose Estimation from Active LED Markers with Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09520)
*Runze Yuan,Alexander Kappler,Jun Zhang,Kuangyi Chen,Fabio Morbidi,Pascal Vasseur,Cédric Demonceaux,Friedrich Fraundorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅需两个LED标记即可估计相机位姿的最小求解器，通过融合IMU/高度计提供的倾斜角和相机高度信息，比传统PnP方法更节省空间且精度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机主动标记定位方法通常依赖PnP求解器，需要至少3个标记或结构化布局，在空间受限场景中部署困难，而利用已有板载传感器提供的部分自运动信息（如重力方向和高度）可以减少标记数量需求。

Method: 推导了一个利用已知倾斜角和相机高度的稳健最小求解器，仅需两个LED标记即可确定相机位姿，同时提供闭式解和线性最小二乘两种求解方案，并分析了退化构型及高度信息对旋转估计无贡献的条件。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据实验中，该方法相比最先进的P2P求解器精度更高，与P3P性能相当。作者开发了基于事件相机和运动捕捉系统的真实数据采集平台进行验证。

Conclusion: 通过融合低成本传感器（IMU/高度计）信息，仅用两个标记即可实现高精度相机位姿估计，为空间受限场景下的实时定位提供了有效替代方案。

Abstract: In many autonomous applications requiring real-time localization, active marker-based systems are preferred due to their low latency and ease of deployment compared to computationally demanding feature-based methods. Event~\mbox{cameras} offer high temporal resolution and minimal delay and are commonly used with active LED markers for robust real-time localization. Existing methods typically rely on Perspective-n-Point (PnP) solvers for pose estimation. However, structured marker layouts can be challenging to deploy in space-constrained scenarios, while partial self-motion information (e.g., gravity direction and altitude) is readily available from onboard sensors. We derive a robust and accurate minimal solver that estimates camera pose from only two LED markers by incorporating known tilt angle and camera height measured by an onboard sensor, such as an IMU or an altimeter. The proposed formulation uniquely determines the camera pose through both a closed-form and a linear least-squares solution. We further analyze degenerate configurations and characterize the conditions under which height information does not contribute to rotation estimation. For evaluation, we developed an event-based active marker system to collect real-world data with ground truth from a motion capture system. Experiments on both synthetic and real data demonstrate improved accuracy over the state-of-the-art P2P solver and competitive performance relative to P3P.

</details>


### [225] [TriView-YOLO: Early Multi-View Fusion for Ground Penetrating Radar Cavity Detection in Soft, High-Water-Content Soils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09522)
*Suphawut Thawinutchokaudom,Sompote Youwai,Warat Kongkitkul,Mitsumasa Yamashina,Jose M. D. S. Rodrigues Neto,Jun Shinohara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TriView-YOLO，一种基于YOLOv12的多视角目标检测器，用于在软弱、高含水量的地面条件下自动检测探地雷达（GPR）图像中的地下空洞。该方法将纵向B扫描、水平C扫描和横截面B扫描三个共配准视角融合为9通道输入，通过TripleInputConv层替换YOLOv12的stem层。在曼谷软海相黏土（含水量80-140%）实地采集测试集上达到mAP50 0.558，消融实验表明辅助视角有效，而公开数据、合成图像、DINOv3特征及更大模型规模均无增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在软弱、高含水量的地面条件下，探地雷达信号被导电的饱和土壤严重衰减，空洞反射信号退化，使得自动检测地下空洞极为困难——而恰恰是这种条件下空洞最易形成。现有研究缺乏针对此类特殊地质条件的深度学习空洞检测评估，需要一种能够在这种恶劣信号环境中有效工作的检测方法。

Method: TriView-YOLO基于YOLOv12架构，将纵向B扫描、水平C扫描和横截面B扫描三种共配准视图组成9通道输入，通过TripleInputConv层替换原有stem层实现多视角特征融合，其余网络结构保持不变，仅在纵向视图上标注边界框。训练采用1,600个专家验证的现场样本（主要来自曼谷城市道路勘测，辅以日本的较硬路基数据），测试集全部来自曼谷软黏土路段。

Result: 在未经增强、纯现场数据的测试集上（曼谷软海相黏土，含水量80-140%，地下水位1-2米），该模型在三个随机种子下达到mAP50为0.558±0.028，计算量为23.6 GFLOPs，单张图像推理时间3.1毫秒。消融实验显示去除辅助视角会降低mAP50和召回率，而公开/合成训练图像、DINOv3特征、更大模型规模及COCO预训练均无性能提升。

Conclusion: TriView-YOLO通过多视角融合有效改善了软质高含水地面下的空洞检测性能，验证了辅助视角信息在信号退化环境中的关键价值。同时揭示了对该特殊场景而言，专用野外数据比公开或合成数据更具价值，模型规模与预训练策略并非性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Automated detection of subsurface cavities from Ground Penetrating Radar (GPR) is most difficult in soft, high-water-content ground, where conductive, water-saturated soil attenuates the signal and degrades cavity reflections, yet this is also the condition under which cavities most readily form. This paper proposes TriView-YOLO, a multi-view YOLOv12 detector for road cavity screening in such ground. Three co-registered views (longitudinal B-scan, horizontal C-scan, and cross-section B-scan) form a 9-channel input fused by a TripleInputConv layer that replaces the YOLOv12 stem; the rest of the network is unchanged, and bounding boxes are required on the longitudinal view only. Training used 1,600 expert-verified field samples, principally metropolitan road surveys of Bangkok, Thailand, acquired with a vehicle-mounted multichannel three-dimensional GPR mobile mapping system, with surveys over the firmer subgrades of Japan added to training and validation only. The test set comes exclusively from the Bangkok surveys, over soft marine clay with 80-140% water content and a water table at 1-2 m depth, a ground condition for which no dedicated deep learning cavity-detection evaluation has been reported. On this unaugmented, field-only test set, split randomly within surveys, the proposed model attains mAP50 of 0.558 +/- 0.028 over three seeds at 23.6 GFLOPs and 3.1 ms per image. Ablations show that removing the auxiliary views lowers mAP50 and recall, whereas public and synthetic training images, DINOv3 features, larger model scale, and COCO pretraining bring no gain.

</details>


### [226] [Towards Expressive and Faithful Audio-to-Image Generation: A Unified Multimodal Dataset and Synthesis Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09529)
*Dongxu Ge,Shansong Liu,Cheng Gong,Xiao-Lei Zhang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了A2I-Set数据集和AudioCanvas模型，解决音频到图像生成任务中数据集质量不足和跨模态对齐困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频到图像（A2I）生成任务受限于传统数据集缺乏高保真图像和精确的跨模态对齐，导致即使微调强大的文本到图像（T2I）模型也难以实现高质量的音频到图像生成。

Method: 构建了包含323K配对的音频、图像和详细文本描述的统一高质量三模态数据集A2I-Set，并通过人工监督开发了混合来源的测试集，在此基础上微调提出AudioCanvas模型。

Result: 实验表明AudioCanvas在视觉表现力和跨模态对齐方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 高质量的三模态数据集和基于该数据集微调的模型能够有效提升音频到图像生成的质量，数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: As an important subfield of cross-modal generation, synthesizing static visual content in the form of images from audio, namely audio-to-image (A2I) generation, has attracted increasing research attention in recent years. Nevertheless, despite the remarkable visual quality of modern text-to-image (T2I) models, the performance of A2I remains fundamentally limited by traditional datasets, which often lack both high-fidelity images and precise cross-modal alignment. As a result, existing methods still struggle to achieve high-quality audio-to-image generation through finetuning strong T2I models, thereby constraining practical applications in this area. Motivated by this gap, we introduce A2I-Set, a unified, high-quality tri-modal dataset consisting of 323K paired audio, images, and detailed text captions, specifically designed for audio-visual research, including audio-conditioned image generation. Besides, we developed a new mixed-source test set for the A2I task through human supervision. We further propose an A2I model, AudioCanvas, fine-tuned on our A2I-Set. Experiments show that AudioCanvas achieves more visually expressive as well as cross-modal alignment results that generally outperforming existing approaches. Our dataset and source code are available at https://github.com/gdx012/A2I-Generation.

</details>


### [227] [DocPure: Prompt-Free Unified Document Restoration via Degradation-Aware Structure-Guided Wavelet Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09536)
*Lingming Su,Wanglong Lu,Tao Wang,Kaihao Zhang,Nan Zhang,Liyan An,Hanli Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DocPure是一个无提示词（prompt-free）的统一文档图像恢复框架，通过退化感知结构自编码器和结构引导的小波交互机制，实现去模糊、去噪、压缩伪影消除和去阴影等多种退化任务的联合恢复，无需推理时的任务提示，仅需训练时的退化标签辅助监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量文档图像对信息存档和下游自动处理至关重要，但采集和传输过程中常受多种退化影响。现有统一文档恢复方法存在三个限制：需要训练多个退化专用模型、依赖手动任务提示、或要求跨任务数据配对。作者旨在提出一个无提示词、无需跨任务配对的统一文档恢复框架。

Method: 1) 设计退化感知结构自编码器（degradation-aware structure auto-encoder），结合退化信息路由正则化（degradation-informed routing regularization），从退化输入中预测干净的structural priors；推理阶段无提示词，退化标签仅作为训练时路由正则化的辅助监督。2) 提出结构引导的小波交互机制（structure-guided wavelet interaction mechanism），桥接频域特征与空间语义；其中跨频自适应调制利用低频子带调制高频恢复，确保结构一致性。

Result: 在去模糊、去噪、压缩伪影消除和去阴影等多个任务上，DocPure相比现有最先进方法取得了更强的性能。

Conclusion: DocPure通过退化感知结构先验和小波频域交互机制，成功实现了无提示词、端到端的统一文档图像恢复，有效解决了现有统一恢复框架在模型训练、提示依赖和数据配对方面的局限。

Abstract: High-quality document images are pivotal for information archiving and downstream automatic processing. However, they are frequently compromised by diverse degradations during uncontrolled acquisition and transmission. While unified document restoration techniques have been proposed to restore images from multiple degradations, they often struggle with training multiple degradation-specific models, reliance on manual task-specific prompts, or cross-task data pairing. To address these limitations, we propose DocPure, a prompt-free unified framework that achieves degradation-aware document restoration.
  We design a degradation-aware structure auto-encoder with degradation-informed routing regularization to predict clean structural priors from degraded inputs. The model is prompt-free at inference, and degradation labels are only used as auxiliary supervision for the routing regularization during training.
  Furthermore, we introduce a structure-guided wavelet interaction mechanism to bridge frequency-domain features and spatial semantics. Within the structure-guided wavelet interaction mechanism, a cross-frequency adaptive modulation utilizes low-frequency sub-bands to modulate high-frequency recovery, ensuring structural consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DocPure achieves strong performance compared with state-of-the-art methods across various tasks, including deblurring, denoising, compression artifact reduction, and deshadowing.

</details>


### [228] [Towards Collaborative Joint Perception and Prediction: Framework, Baseline Evaluation, and Deployment Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09541)
*Lei Wan,Hannan Ejaz Keen,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了协作联合感知与预测（Co-P&P）范式，将协作感知与运动预测统一起来，以缓解感知误差累积和视觉遮挡问题。实验表明，预测级融合性能不如检测级或跟踪级融合；基于RENO神经编码器和FutureDet的端到端原型显示，协作能提升预测精度，同时神经压缩可在约34倍更低的通信带宽下保持这一优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAVs利用V2X通信进行协作感知，但现有CP能力仍面临感知误差累积和视觉遮挡两大挑战，需要将感知与预测统一起来以提升对周围交通参与者的运动预测能力和态势感知水平。

Method: 提出Co-P&P概念框架，在前期研究基础上对比不同融合策略（预测级、检测级、跟踪级）的性能；实现一个最小端到端Co-P&P原型，将RENO神经编码器用于协作点云共享，结合FutureDet实现联合检测-预测。

Result: 预测级融合相比检测级或跟踪级融合会导致整体系统性能下降；端到端原型表明协作能改善预测精度，而RENO神经压缩在约34倍更低通信带宽下仍能保持协作带来的性能优势。

Conclusion: Co-P&P是提升CAV在复杂动态交通环境中态势感知能力的有前景范式，检测级/跟踪级融合优于预测级融合，且通过高效协作点云压缩可在低带宽下实现协作预测的收益。

Abstract: Connected Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) increasingly exploit Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communication to exchange multi-source sensor information, enabling advanced Collaborative Perception (CP) capabilities. Extending beyond these capabilities, this work focuses on Collaborative Joint Perception and Prediction (Co-P&P), a paradigm that unifies CP with motion prediction to mitigate two persistent challenges: the accumulation of perception errors and visual occlusions. We present a conceptual framework for Collaborative Joint Perception and Prediction (Co-P&P) that improves motion prediction of surrounding road users, thereby enhancing situational awareness in complex and dynamic traffic environments. Building upon our preliminary study, this extended version compares the performance of different fusion strategies and establishes baseline performance for a modular design of perception and prediction. Experimental results show that prediction-level fusion leads to a decline in overall system performance compared to detection-level or tracking-level fusion. We further implement a minimal end-to-end Co-P&P prototype that couples collaborative point-cloud sharing via the RENO neural codec with joint detection-forecasting via FutureDet, showing that collaboration improves forecasting accuracy while neural compression preserves this benefit at roughly 34x lower communication bandwidth.

</details>


### [229] [PressureMesh: 3D Human Mesh Estimation from Multi-Device Pressure Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09550)
*Changhai Ma,Ziyu Wu,Yunkang Zhang,Fangting Xie,Mengting Niu,Heyu Ding,Quan Wan,Jiayue Yuan,Boyan Liu,Yi Ke,Xiaohui Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MDP-Net，一种端到端网络，能够从多设备时空压力数据中直接估计人体网格，解决现有基于压力的人体姿态监测方法仅限单设备、监测范围受限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于压力的人体姿态监测方法受限于单一设备，导致有效监测范围有限，无法满足康复评估和人机交互等场景对更大范围、非侵入式监测的需求。

Method: 提出MDP-Net端到端网络，借鉴混合专家（MoE）框架设计多模态融合机制，实现跨设备压力信息的有效互补与增强；同时构建了包含2D/3D关节点和人体网格等多类姿态标签的高质量多设备时空压力数据集MDP。

Result: MDP-Net在MDP数据集上实现了12.6cm的关节点位置误差，验证了融合多设备压力信息进行日常人体姿态监测的有效性。

Conclusion: 融合多设备压力信息是日常人体姿态监测的一种有效且有前景的新方案，能够克服单设备监测范围的局限。

Abstract: Human pose monitoring is crucial in fields such as rehabilitation assessment and human-computer interaction. Due to its privacy-preserving nature, pressure-based human pose monitoring has become a primary approach for unobtrusive sensing. However, existing methods are generally limited to a single device, which restricts the effective monitoring range. To address this limitation, we propose MDP-Net, an end-to-end network capable of directly estimating human meshes from temporal pressure data across multiple devices. We introduce a multimodal fusion mechanism inspired by the Mixture of Experts (MoE) framework to achieve effective complementarity and enhancement of cross-device pressure information. To support the training and evaluation of MDP-Net, we constructed MDP, a high-quality multi-device temporal pressure dataset that includes various pose labels such as 2D/3D joints and human meshes. Experimental results demonstrate that MDP-Net achieves a joint position error of 12.6 cm on the MDP dataset. These results prove that fusing multi-device pressure information is an effective and promising new solution for daily human pose monitoring.

</details>


### [230] [From Semantic Grounding to Decision Optimization: A Unified Framework for Long-Horizon UAV Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09564)
*Zeyuan Ma,Jiaxin Chen,Di Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向UAV视觉-语言导航的统一"语义到决策"框架，通过指令接地语义增强、相关性感知动态时间聚合和拓扑感知决策方法，在AerialVLN和OpenFly基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN方法存在三个耦合问题：指令相关地标在视觉观测中接地能力弱、长时序历史信息利用不足、在局部陷阱或重复探索中决策不稳定。

Method: 提出统一框架，包含三个模块：(1)指令接地语义增强模块，将对象级语义和相对空间线索注入当前观测状态；(2)相关性感知动态时间聚合策略，对完整历史缓冲区重新加权，并将少量高相关帧转换为结构化地标提示；(3)拓扑感知决策方法，结合局部最优认知与基于进度、目标、语义和路径合规奖励的组相对策略优化。

Result: 在AerialVLN和OpenFly两个广泛使用的基准上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 该框架通过语义增强和拓扑感知决策有效解决了UAV-VLN中的三大耦合问题，验证了方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: UAV vision-language navigation (UAV-VLN) focuses on enabling an aerial agent to follow natural-language instructions in open 3D environments from egocentric visual observations. Current approaches suffer from three coupled issues: weak grounding of instruction-relevant landmarks in visual observations, insufficient exploitation of long-horizon history, and unstable decisions under local traps or repeated exploration. To address these issues, we propose a unified semantic-to-decision framework. First, we present an instruction-grounded semantic enhancement module that injects object-level semantics and relative spatial cues into the current observation state. Subsequently, we develop a relevance-aware dynamic temporal aggregation strategy that reweights the full history buffer while converting a few high-relevance frames into structured landmark prompts for the decoder. Finally, we devise a topology-aware decision method that combines local-optimum cognition with group-relative policy optimization under progress, goal, semantic, and path-compliance rewards. Experiments on the widely used AerialVLN and OpenFly benchmarks clearly demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [231] [VideoVIBE: A Video-Grounded Diagnostic Benchmark for One-Shot Interactive Website Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09573)
*Jiajun Xu,Yanghao Zhou,Jingyun Liao,Yu Bai,Jinxing Zhou,Chengliang Liu,Changsen Yuan,Bo Wang,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoVIBE基准，将人工操作的网页录屏转化为约1.7K细粒度诊断性视频QA实例，覆盖6,338个已验证的网页生成失败案例；同时提出V2Lens多智能体系统，通过在十三种视频多模态大模型上取得71.72分（较最强基线提升7.18分），论证了基于视频的评估能提供行为忠实且具有诊断信息的生成应用质量评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言驱动的"vibe coding"能够一次性生成视觉丰富且交互式的网页应用，但其质量的可靠评估尚未跟上。现有评估方法通常只对孤立产物或最终任务结果打分，难以揭示具体失败类型及成因，缺乏行为层面的诊断证据。

Method: （1）构建VideoVIBE基准：将人工操作的网页录屏转换为细粒度诊断任务，包含约1.7K个视频QA实例，源自6,338个已验证失败，涵盖语义逻辑、视觉运动、结构时序和功能四类失败，以录屏展示和交互行为为主要依据，源码作为补充上下文。（2）提出V2Lens：一个免训练、基于证据的多智能体系统，通过针对性的视觉和源码验证来质疑并选择性修正初始基于视频的诊断结论。

Result: 在十三个闭源和开源视频多模态大模型上，Gemini-2.5-Flash是最强的独立模型，得分64.54；V2Lens达到71.72分，较基线提升7.18分。

Conclusion: 视频维度的评估能够超越孤立产物和聚合结果，提供行为上忠实且诊断信息丰富的生成应用质量评价，为vibe coding生成的网页应用质量评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Natural-language-driven "vibe coding" enables the one-shot generation of visually rich and interactive web applications, yet reliable assessment of their quality has not kept pace. Existing evaluations often score isolated artifacts or final task outcomes, offering limited evidence about which failures occur and why. We introduce VideoVIBE, a video-grounded benchmark that transforms human-operated webpage recordings into fine-grained diagnostic tasks. It contains approximately 1.7K diagnostic Video QA instances derived from 6,338 verified failures across generated webpages, spanning semantic-logical, visual-motion, structural-temporal, and functional failures. Diagnoses are grounded primarily in recorded presentation and behavior, with webpage source code used as complementary context. We further propose V2Lens, a training-free, evidence-grounded multi-agent system that challenges and selectively refines initial video-based diagnoses through targeted visual and source-code verification. Across thirteen closed-source and open-weight Video MLLMs, Gemini-2.5-Flash is the strongest standalone model with a score of 64.54, while V2Lens reaches 71.72, an improvement of 7.18 points. Together, our results show that video-grounded evaluation can move beyond isolated artifacts and aggregate outcomes toward a behaviorally faithful and diagnostically informative account of generated application quality.

</details>


### [232] [MSP-Net: Manifold-Guided Spectral Prompt Network for Hyperspectral Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09575)
*Juliu Li,Hanlin Qin,Shuowen Yang,Jingjing Li,Yuedong Tan,Shuai Yuan,Huixin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MSP-Net（流形引导光谱提示网络），通过图驱动的流形路由重构波段关系并形成自适应光谱分组，结合分组光谱统计与模板外观构建动态条件提示，实现跨传感器环境下高光谱目标跟踪的鲁棒性提升。在HOT2020和HOT2023上AUC超过0.80，Precision超过0.96。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱目标跟踪方法通常将高光谱图像简单视为RGB的多通道扩展，在固定波段顺序下进行特征融合，导致模型依赖特定传感器配置、忽视波段间的流形关系，难以泛化到异构传感器。同时，波段的判别贡献会随目标属性和场景变化而动态改变，静态融合策略的表达能力受限。

Method: 提出MSP-Net，包括三个核心模块：（1）图驱动流形路由，重构波段关系并形成自适应光谱分组；（2）将分组光谱统计与模板外观联合集成，构建目标相关的动态条件提示，增强目标特征并抑制背景干扰；（3）利用中间目标表示持续更新光谱条件，并结合可靠历史状态约束目标定位和尺度波动，提升跨传感器跟踪的时序稳定性。

Result: 在HOT2020和HOT2023数据集上，MSP-Net的AUC超过0.80，Precision超过0.96，在异构传感器、目标形变和复杂背景条件下展现出卓越的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MSP-Net通过流形引导的自适应光谱分组和动态条件提示机制，有效解决了静态波段融合在跨传感器高光谱跟踪中的泛化瓶颈，为高光谱目标跟踪提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Hyperspectral object tracking leverages abundant spectral information to provide unique advantages for target discrimination in complex scenes. However, existing methods typically treat hyperspectral images as multi-channel extensions of RGB images, performing feature fusion in fixed band order. This approach leads to models dependent on specific sensor configurations while neglecting manifold relationships between bands, making generalization to heterogeneous sensors difficult. Moreover, the discriminative contribution of bands dynamically changes with target attributes and scene variations, further limiting the representational capacity of static fusion strategies. To address this, we propose the Manifold-Guided Spectral Prompt Network (MSP-Net). This network first reconstructs band relationships and forms adaptive spectral grouping through graph-driven manifold routing, then jointly integrates grouped spectral statistics with template appearance to construct target-related dynamic conditional prompts, enhancing target features while suppressing background interference. Furthermore, as tracking progresses, spectral conditions continuously evolve based on intermediate target representations, enabling target prompts to adapt in real-time to appearance and scene changes. Meanwhile, reliable historical states are used to constrain target localization and scale fluctuations, significantly improving temporal stability in cross-sensor tracking. Experiments on HOT2020 and HOT2023 demonstrate that MSP-Net achieves AUC and Precision exceeding 0.80 and 0.96, respectively, exhibiting exceptional robustness under heterogeneous sensors, target deformation, and complex background conditions. The code will be released at https://github.com/GGML668897/MSP-Net.

</details>


### [233] [You Only Flow Once: Calibrated and Real-Time Radar Pose Estimation with Multi-Hypothesis Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09579)
*Jonas Leo Mueller,Sebastian Hoefler,Dario Zanca,Naga Venkata Sai Jitin Jami,Thomas Altstidl,Bjoern M. Eskofier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MH-NFPG，一种基于条件归一化流的多假设人体姿态生成器，用于毫米波雷达点云的姿态估计。它通过时空Transformer骨干与归一化流结合，将Laplace基础分布转化为表达性后验分布，单次前向传播即可并行生成多个姿态假设，在三个雷达基准上优于扩散模型，推理速度提升20倍以上，校准误差降低最多85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达点云稀疏且带噪，同一观测可能对应多种合理的人体姿态，导致确定性姿态估计本质上是病态问题。现有雷达方法仍采用确定性估计，将这种多义性坍缩为单一结果；而基于扩散模型的方案虽能建模多假设分布，但需要昂贵的顺序去噪过程且缺乏校准的不确定性。

Method: MH-NFPG将时空Transformer骨干与条件归一化流结合，通过归一化流将Laplace基础分布变换为表达性后验分布，整个生成过程通过单次前向传播并行完成，避免了扩散模型逐样本顺序去噪的低效问题。

Result: 在MM-Fi、mmRadPose和mRI三个雷达基准上，MH-NFPG在校准方面优于基于扩散的替代方法，在两个基准上改善了姿态精度并在第三个上与其持平；推理速度提升20倍以上，校准误差最多降低85%。同时发现扩散模型在校准上显著退化，而基于流的方法在跨环境场景中仍保持可靠的覆盖。

Conclusion: 归一化流可以作为扩散模型的实用替代方案，用于实时、不确定性感知的雷达姿态估计，在效率与校准性能之间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: Sparse and noisy millimeter-wave radar point cloud observations often correspond to multiple plausible human poses, making deterministic pose estimation fundamentally ill-posed. Yet existing radar methods remain deterministic, collapsing this ambiguity into a single estimate. Diffusion-based alternatives can model multi-hypothesis distributions but require costly sequential denoising for each distribution sample and lack calibrated uncertainty. We propose Multi-Hypothesis Normalizing Flow Pose Generator (MH-NFPG), which models pose distributions from radar point clouds using a conditional normalizing flow. Specifically, we combine a spatiotemporal transformer backbone with a normalizing flow that transforms a Laplace base distribution into an expressive posterior, generated in parallel through a single forward pass. Leveraging this efficiency, we outperform diffusion-based alternatives in calibration across three radar benchmarks (MM-Fi, mmRadPose, mRI), improve pose accuracy on two, and match it on the third, while achieving over 20x faster inference for applications and reducing calibration error by up to 85%. We find that calibration degrades substantially for diffusion models, whereas our flow-based approach maintains reliable coverage, also in cross-environment settings. These results demonstrate normalizing flows as a practical alternative to diffusion models for real-time, uncertainty-aware radar pose estimation. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [234] [GenTrack3: Hybrid Stochastic-Deterministic Online Multi-Object Tracking with Cluster-Aware Association](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09581)
*Toan Van Nguyen,Rasmus G. K. Christiansen,Dirk Kraft,Leon Bodenhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线多目标跟踪框架，融合确定性（tracking-by-detection + 数据关联）与随机性（粒子滤波）方法，通过新型轨迹-检测匹配策略提升可扩展性，并支持群体跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性多目标跟踪方法依赖运动模型且对噪声检测敏感，容易产生关联错误；随机方法能更好处理非线性动态和不确定性，但计算开销大且因随机采样导致结果波动。现有单一范式难以在鲁棒性和效率之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出一个在线MOT框架，融合确定性与随机性原理。引入一种新颖的轨迹-检测匹配方法以增强目标数量增长时的可扩展性并支持群体跟踪。采用包含标识、状态、速度、轨迹惩罚和轨迹年龄的tracklet作为跟踪推理机制，每个目标关联一个随机粒子集用于计算与检测的匹配代价。

Result: 论文提供了参考实现和基线跟踪器代码（GitHub），具体定量结果需参阅全文。

Conclusion: 融合确定性数据关联与随机粒子推理的混合方法，能够在不确定性条件下实现鲁棒跟踪，同时通过改进的匹配策略保证可扩展性和群体跟踪能力。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) involves maintaining consistent target identities as objects dynamically enter and leave a scene. Deterministic approaches, such as tracking-by-detection with data association, produce reproducible results and are computationally efficient, but they rely heavily on motion models and are sensitive to noisy detections that can lead to association errors. In contrast, stochastic methods explicitly model uncertainty and can better handle complex non-linear dynamics, albeit at the cost of increased computational complexity and variability arising from random sampling. This paper presents an online MOT framework that integrates deterministic and stochastic principles to achieve robust tracking under uncertainty. Furthermore, a novel track-to-detection matching approach is introduced to enhance scalability with increasing target numbers while supporting group tracking. The tracking inference mechanism employs a tracklet that includes identifiers, states, velocities, track penalties and track ages of targets, supporting a systematic tracking pipeline. Each target is associated with a stochastic particle set to compute the matching cost to detections. Reference implementations of the proposed approach and baseline trackers can be found on GitHub: https://github.com/SDU-VelKoTek/GenTrack3.

</details>


### [235] [TeaMatch: Teachable Cross-Modal Representation Learning for 2D-3D Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09590)
*Chongjian Wang,Junjie Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TeaMatch提出了一种以"可教学性"为准则的跨模态表示学习框架，通过模拟常见失效模式的弱学生模型来训练教师模型，提升图像与点云之间2D-3D匹配的鲁棒性，无需额外推理成本即可集成到现有匹配流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D-3D检测无关匹配方法主要在同一模型内进行优化，在噪声输入、低重叠度和模糊结构等挑战性条件下难以维持可靠的对应关系，缺乏对跨模态表示鲁棒性的系统性考量。

Method: 将"可教学性"定义为表示在退化输入下能被弱学习者有效恢复的能力。构建一组模拟常见失效模式的任务特定弱学生模型，在训练集上模仿教师，在独立的元分割集上评估其可恢复性；教师通过对应级别和几何感知约束进行优化以提升学生的恢复能力。

Result: TeaMatch可无缝集成到现有粗到细的匹配流程中且不增加推理成本，在多个具有挑战性的2D-3D匹配基准上提升了匹配鲁棒性并达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入可教学性作为跨模态表示学习的新准则，TeaMatch有效提升了2D-3D匹配在复杂条件下的鲁棒性，验证了元学习式师生框架在跨模态匹配任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Learning reliable correspondences between images and point clouds is fundamental for 2D-3D matching. Despite recent progress in detection-free methods, existing approaches primarily optimize matching within a single model and often struggle to maintain reliable correspondences under challenging conditions such as noisy inputs, low overlap, and ambiguous structures. In this work, we propose TeaMatch, a novel framework that introduces teachability as a criterion for cross-modal representation learning. We define teachability as the ability of a representation to be effectively recovered by weak learners under degraded inputs, reflecting its structural consistency and robustness. To this end, we construct a set of task-specific weak students that simulate common failure modes and train them to imitate the teacher on a training split while evaluating their recoverability on a disjoint meta split. The teacher is then optimized to improve the students' ability to recover reliable correspondences, guided by correspondence-level and geometry-aware constraints. Our framework can be seamlessly integrated into existing coarse-to-fine matching pipelines without additional inference cost. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TeaMatch improves matching robustness and achieves state-of-the-art performance on challenging 2D-3D matching benchmarks.

</details>


### [236] [Illusion or Integrity? Geometrical Consistency Metric for AIGC Video Quality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09594)
*Yifei Xue,Yuanchen Fei,Hao Zhang,Chenzhi Nie,Tie ji,Yizhen Lao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoCon-Bench，一个基于物理定律遵从度来评估AI生成视频质量的基准，通过定量测量帧间几何一致性（包括单应性/基础矩阵拟合、内点比例和几何误差）来代理估计生成视频是否符合真实物理规则，并发布了包含6类运动场景共20个场景的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成视频质量评估方法主要关注视觉和谐性、视频-文本一致性和领域特定对齐，但缺乏量化度量视频是否符合物理定律的指标。由于AI生成视频的物理合理性对其可信度和实用性至关重要，因此需要一种能够定量评估生成视频物理保真度的基准。

Method: GeoCon-Bench通过以下步骤实现物理一致性评估：1）从生成视频中提取帧序列；2）通过平移估计捕获全局运动；3）利用背景对应点拟合单应性矩阵或基础矩阵模型；4）报告互补性的指标，包括内点比例（inlier ratio）和几何误差（geometric error），以定量测量帧间的几何一致性。

Result: 在多个最先进的AIGC生成模型上进行实验，结果表明GeoCon-Bench作为视频质量评估指标具有良好的可靠性。此外，发布了包含20个场景、覆盖6种运动类别的数据集。

Conclusion: GeoCon-Bench填补了AI生成视频物理定律合规性量化评估的空白，通过几何一致性测量为AIGC视频质量评估提供了新的、可靠的补充视角，有助于指导生成模型的优化。

Abstract: Recently, AI-driven video generation has attracted considerable attention. This surge increases the demand for reliable video quality assessment (VQA) metrics to evaluate AI-generated content (AIGC) videos and guide model optimization. Existing studies assess video quality through visual harmony, video-text consistency, and domain-specific alignment, yet lack quantitative metrics for measuring fidelity to physical laws. To address this limitation, we present a novel benchmark that evaluates the quality of AIGC videos based on their compliance with physical principles by quantitatively measuring geometric consistency across frames extracted from generated sequences. This serves as a proxy for estimating the extent to which generated videos conform to real-world physical rules. Specifically, GeoCon-Bench captures global motion through translation estimation, fits homography or fundamental matrix models using background correspondences, and reports complementary metrics, including inlier ratio and geometric error. We also release a dataset containing 20 scenes across six motion categories. Experiments on state-of-the-art AIGC models demonstrate the reliability of GeoCon-Bench as a video quality assessment metric.

</details>


### [237] [ResemBrick: Brick Reconstruction from Photographs with Perceptual Fidelity and Buildability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09597)
*Xilun Chen,Hanwen Wan,Yusong Zhao,Zexin Lin,Ruixiang Liao,Xiaoqiang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ResemBrick提出了一种从少量照片生成可手工搭建的彩色积木模型的完整方法，将体素离散化与物理可搭建性紧密耦合。通过预算受限的占用补全网络（单一权重覆盖13种分辨率）一次性决定哪些表面体素被填充以最大化观感，同时采用支持感知和前视贪心放置策略结合确定性修复算法保证零漂浮、零不稳定积木。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有积木生成管线要么只优化视觉相似度，要么只优化结构稳定性；且都将体素化视为固定的预处理步骤而非可优化的变量。作者认为这是一个更广泛挑战的测试平台：在离散、预算受限的体素网格下生成满足硬性物理装配约束的3D内容。

Method: 1) 预算受限的占用补全网络：将离散化重新表述为分配问题，给定目标占用体素数，一个分辨率条件化网络在单次前向传播中决定哪些表面体素应填充，单一权重集覆盖13种分辨率。2) 可搭建性构造：采用支持感知和前视感知的贪心放置策略，配合确定性且可证明终止的修复算法，将每个漂浮部件接地。

Result: 在匹配预算下，ResemBrick在感知保真度上超越现有体素选择器，并在未过滤的留出对象上唯一实现零漂浮和零不稳定积木；作为完整管线，在既有积木构建系统中取得最优感知保真度。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，应把离散化和装配视为紧密耦合的阶段而非相互独立的环节，这为物理约束下的3D内容生成提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Producing a hand-buildable, colored brick model of a 3D object from a few casual photographs is a clean testbed for a broader challenge: generating 3D content that meets hard physical-assembly constraints under a discrete, budget-limited voxel grid. On a coarse lattice, visual resemblance and structural stability pull against each other, yet prior brick pipelines address only one side and treat voxelization as fixed preprocessing rather than a variable to optimize. We present ResemBrick, which couples the two. Budgeted occupancy completion reframes discretization as allocation: given a target occupied-voxel count, a single resolution-conditioned network decides in one feed-forward pass which surface voxels to fill for best appearance, one weight set spanning 13 resolutions. Buildability by construction then combines support- and look-ahead-aware greedy placement with a deterministic, provably terminating repair that grounds every floating component. Under a matched budget, ResemBrick surpasses existing voxel selectors in perceptual fidelity while uniquely reaching zero floating and zero unstable bricks on unfiltered held-out objects; as a complete pipeline, it attains the best perceptual fidelity among prior brick-construction systems. Our results point to treating discretization and assembly as tightly coupled stages rather than independent ones.

</details>


### [238] [Structure-Enhanced Features and Quality-Aware Dynamic Anchor Scoring for Robust Lane Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09610)
*Weize Cai,Yongqi Dong,Zhida Shao,Yichen Liu,Zixin Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构增强与质量感知的车道检测框架，通过GHVT模块增强骨干网络特征连续性，以及LQAS模块校准分类置信度与定位质量的一致性，在保持ADNet推理流程不变的前提下，在VIL-100、CULane和TuSimple等数据集上显著提升F1分数，同时降低误检与漏检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锚点式车道检测器面临两个耦合问题：一是骨干网络特征在部分可见车道区域缺乏结构连续性；二是分类置信度与线级定位质量可能解耦，导致不准确的锚点在NMS之前持续存在，限制了检测性能。

Method: 提出两个核心模块：(1) Gated Horizontal-Vertical Token (GHVT) 模块，通过轻量级方向性token交互和可学习残差门控增强中高层骨干特征；(2) Line-Quality-Aware Dynamic Anchor Scoring (LQAS)，利用质量监督、难负样本抑制和成对排序来校准现有分类logits，无需增加推理分支。

Result: 在VIL-100数据集上，ADNet-R34的F1@50从89.97提升至91.28，同时减少假阳性和假阴性；在CULane和TuSimple数据集上的实验、大量消融研究、分数分布诊断和运行时分析均确认了结构增强与排序改进的互补效果，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持ADNet推理管线完全不变的前提下，通过特征结构增强和动态锚点质量评分双管齐下，有效解决了车道检测中部分可见车道结构断裂和锚点评分失准的问题，以极小的额外计算代价实现了一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Lane detection requires recovering thin, elongated, and frequently occluded lane structures under challenging driving conditions. While anchor-based detectors provide efficient candidate generation, their performance is limited by two coupled issues: backbone features often lose structural continuity along partially visible lanes, and classification confidence may decouple from line-level localization quality, allowing inaccurate anchors to persist before non-maximum suppression (NMS). We propose a structure-enhanced and quality-aware framework that improves lane representation and dynamic-anchor scoring while preserving the inference pipeline of the Anchor Decomposition Network (ADNet). Specifically, a Gated Horizontal-Vertical Token (GHVT) module enhances mid- and high-level backbone features via lightweight directional token interactions with a learnable residual gate. In parallel, Line-Quality-Aware Dynamic Anchor Scoring (LQAS) calibrates existing classification logits using quality supervision, hard-negative suppression, and pairwise ranking without adding inference branches. On the VIL-100 dataset, our method improves ADNet-R34 from 89.97 to 91.28 in F1 score at the 0.5 intersection-over-union threshold (F1@50), reducing both false positives and false negatives. Additional experiments on CULane and TuSimple datasets, extensive ablations, score-distribution diagnostics, and runtime analysis confirm complementary structural and ranking improvements with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [239] [Marrying Optimal Transport and ODEs for Unified Continuous-Time 4D Reconstruction and Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09613)
*Liying Yang,Hao Mo,Jialun Liu,Chen Liu,Xinxing Yu,Chenhao Guan,Hui Ma,Xiao Cao,Ajian Liu,Yanyan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Uni4R框架，通过融合最优传输（OT）和常微分方程（ODE）学习连续速度场，统一4D重建与点跟踪任务，实现任意时间戳下的运动建模，在两项任务及新增的连续时间运动学基准上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一4D重建与点跟踪方法依赖启发式插值或仅在整数时间戳预测，缺乏运动学一致性，无法建模任意时间戳下的动态。

Method: 提出Flow Matching Guided Decoder（FMGD），包含全局速度分支提取锚定特征，利用Flow Matching理论在锚定特征流形上构建由OT定义的概率路径，生成FM引导的速度特征；同时通过点重建分支提供几何特征，局部速度预测模块联合这两类特征与时间嵌入解码任意时间戳的速度。此外提出积分一致性训练策略，用ODE求解器积分速度恢复目标点图，实现端到端监督。

Result: Uni4R在4D重建和点跟踪任务上均达到SOTA性能，并在新提出的连续时间运动学感知基准上也取得SOTA结果。

Conclusion: 通过将连续速度场作为运动学先验，Uni4R成功让4D重建与点跟踪两个任务相互促进，证明了OT与ODE协同在统一动态建模任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Existing unified 4D reconstruction and point tracking approaches typically rely on heuristic interpolations or just predict at integer timestamps, lacking kinematic coherence and failing to model dynamics at any arbitrary timestamp. In this paper, we propose Uni4R, a framework that unifies these tasks by learning continuous velocity fields through the synergy of Optimal Transport (OT) and Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE). Importantly, this continuous velocity field acts as a kinematic prior that mutually benefits both 4D reconstruction and point tracking. Specifically, we propose the Flow Matching Guided Decoder (FMGD). A global velocity branch first extracts anchor features that capture the global dynamic state of the sequence. Then, FMGD leverages Flow Matching (FM) theory to formulate a probability path defined by OT on the anchor feature manifold, instantiating it as FM-guided velocity features for velocity prediction. This establishes a robust kinematic inductive bias. Meanwhile, a point reconstruction branch provides geometric features. The local velocity prediction module then joint above features and time embeddings, to decode velocities at arbitrary timestamps. To overcome the absence of high-quality ground-truth velocities in fractional frames, we propose an integral-consistency training strategy. This strategy uses an ODE solver to integrate velocities to recover target pointmaps, enabling the model to be supervised end-to-end directly from integer timestamps. Experimental results demonstrate that Uni4R achieves SOTA performance in both 4D reconstruction and point tracking, and achieves SOTA in our new kinematics-aware benchmark at continuous time.

</details>


### [240] [LoRA-based Adaptation Alone Is Not Enough: Understanding the Limits of Foundation Models for Face Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09633)
*Peter Lorenz,Anjith George,Marcel Sébastien*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了32种基础模型（FMs）用于人脸呈现攻击检测（PAD）的效果，发现零样本提示性能接近随机，而低秩适配（LoRA）在数据集内能达到低于2%的ACER，但跨数据集泛化能力仍然有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PAD系统大多仅聚焦于CLIP模型，忽略了其他架构和训练方式不同的基础模型；同时跨数据集评估下PAD性能大幅下降，需要探索更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 在MCIO基准（MSU-MFSD、CASIA-FASD、Replay-Attack、OULU-NPU）上系统评估32种基础模型，比较零样本提示与低秩适配（LoRA，<1%可训练参数）两种策略在数据集内和跨数据集场景下的性能。

Result: 零样本提示性能接近随机水平；LoRA适配后大多数模型在数据集内ACER低于2%，但跨数据集ACER显著更高。LoRA主要是在数据集内细化决策边界，对跨数据集泛化帮助有限。

Conclusion: 预训练表示和适配数据集对跨数据集泛化的影响大于轻量级适配策略本身；单一轻量适配方法难以解决PAD的跨数据集退化问题。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection (PAD) aims to reliably detect a wide range of presentation attacks. While PAD methods achieve strong performance within individual datasets, their performance degrades under cross-dataset evaluation. Variations in sensors or lighting conditions can reduce the effectiveness of detectors from near-perfect to nearly random. Foundation models (FMs) have emerged as a promising alternative because typical PAD datasets, such as the MCIO benchmarks (MSU-MFSD, CASIA-FASD, Replay-Attack, and OULU-NPU), are small relative to the scale used for web-based pretraining. However, existing PAD systems primarily focus on CLIP-based foundation models, while overlooking other FMs with different architectures and training procedures. This study addresses this question by systematically evaluating 32 FMs. Zero-shot prompting achieves performance near chance across model families and scales. The vision encoders, when low-rankadapted (LoRA) with fewer than 1% trainable weights, achieve below 2% intra-dataset ACER in most cases, while cross-dataset ACER is substantially higher. LoRA primarily refines the decision boundary within a dataset, suggesting that pretrained representations and the adaptation dataset play a larger role in cross-dataset generalization than the evaluated lightweight adaptation strategy.

</details>


### [241] [NeuroRefiner: Morphology-Aware Multi-Agent Refinement for 3D Fluorescence Microscopy Neuron Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09636)
*Haiyang Yan,Jinyue Guo,Yanchao Zhang,Bingqing Wang,Zhenchen Li,Jing Liu,Jiazheng Liu,Linlin Li,Hua Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeuroRefiner，一个多智能体系统，用于荧光显微镜下3D神经元的精确分割。该系统模拟人类专家"全局观察+局部编辑"的工作流程，通过三个协作智能体（拓扑错误诊断、修正指令生成、质量验证）结合TopoRefineNet网络（基于3D U-Net和跨模态特征融合），实现多轮推理和体素级编辑，在BigNeuron、CWMBS和ZBFWB数据集上超越了现有方法，尤其在ZBFWB上F1分数提升3.02%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D神经元分割方法难以同时保留局部细节和全局拓扑结构，导致分割结果碎片化。神经元稀疏且细长的形态给分割带来了独特挑战，而传统方法缺乏对全局拓扑错误的诊断和修正能力。

Method: 提出NeuroRefiner多智能体系统，将人类专家的迭代式"全局观察+局部编辑"工作流形式化。系统包含三个协作智能体：诊断拓扑错误、生成修正指令、验证精炼质量。同时提出TopoRefineNet，一种基于3D U-Net的专用工具，利用跨模态特征融合生成精炼掩膜。通过多轮智能体推理和体素级编辑实现分割精炼。

Result: 在BigNeuron、CWMBS和ZBFWB三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuroRefiner优于最先进方法，特别是在具有挑战性的ZBFWB数据集上实现了3.02%的F1分数提升。

Conclusion: NeuroRefiner通过多智能体协作与指令引导的分割精炼，有效解决了3D神经元分割中的拓扑完整性问题，在提升分割精度的同时增强了结果的可解释性，为神经科学研究提供了更可靠的神经元形态分析工具。

Abstract: Accurate 3D neuron segmentation in fluorescence microscopy is critical for neuroscience. However, the sparse and elongated morphology of neurons poses significant challenges to existing segmentation methods. These methods struggle to preserve both local details and global topology, leading to fragmented results. To address this, we propose NeuroRefiner, a multi-agent system that formalizes the human expert workflow involving iterative global observation and local editing. Specifically, NeuroRefiner comprises three collaborative agents dedicated to diagnosing topological errors, generating correction instructions, and validating refinement quality. To facilitate agent instruction-guided segmentation refinement, we propose TopoRefineNet, a dedicated 3D U-Net-based tool that leverages cross-modality feature fusion to generate refined masks. Through multi-round agent reasoning and voxel-level editing, NeuroRefiner produces topologically more accurate segmentations with enhanced interpretability. Experiments on the BigNeuron, CWMBS, and ZBFWB datasets demonstrate that NeuroRefiner outperforms state-of-the-art methods, notably achieving a 3.02% improvement in F1 score on the challenging ZBFWB dataset.

</details>


### [242] [DUET: A Diversity-Quality Duet of Distillation Experts for Two-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09637)
*Zian Li,Litong Gong,Borui Liao,Pengfei Liu,Xinyu Wang,Xinyuan Wei,Yifan Gao,Tiezheng Ge,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DUET框架，通过噪声级别专家分工（高噪声步用sCM专家保持多样性、低噪声步用DMD专家提升质量），实现两步视频生成中质量与多样性的双赢，并进一步提出DUET+通过RL引导的专家适配解决中继接口和高噪声阶段瓶颈，在Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B骨干上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型实现高质量视频生成但迭代采样成本高；少步蒸馏虽有缓解但存在质量-多样性权衡问题：轨迹级蒸馏（如sCM）偏多样性，分布级蒸馏（如DMD）偏质量，难以两全。

Method: 提出噪声级"专家二重奏"（DUET）架构：sCM专家负责高噪声步以布局多样化结构，DMD专家负责低噪声步以精炼外观细节；两专家独立训练、各用原生目标函数，避免损失级组合的优化困难；进一步提出DUET+，通过RL引导的专家适配来解决中继接口和高噪声阶段这两个剩余瓶颈。

Result: 以Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B为骨干，DUET将两步生成的sCM质量提升至接近DMD水平，同时保留约两倍于DMD的结构多样性；DUET+在保持多样性优势的同时进一步提升整体质量。

Conclusion: 噪声级专家专业化是一种简单有效的范式，能够在两步视频生成中同时实现多样性与质量，而非在两者之间取舍。

Abstract: Diffusion models have enabled high-quality video generation in recent years, but the high cost of iterative sampling hinders their practical deployment. Few-step distillation alleviates this cost, yet exposes a quality--diversity trade-off between its two dominant paradigms: trajectory-level distillation (e.g., sCM) favors diversity, whereas distribution-level distillation (e.g., DMD) favors quality. Targeting extreme two-step video generation, we introduce DUET, which reconciles the two paradigms through a noise-level duet of experts: an sCM expert takes the high-noise step to lay out diverse structure, and a DMD expert takes the low-noise step to refine appearance detail. Since the two experts are trained independently with their native objectives, DUET sidesteps the optimization difficulties of loss-level combinations and delivers quality and diversity jointly rather than trading one for the other. We further identify the relay interface and the high-noise stage as the remaining bottlenecks, and address them with RL-guided expert adaptation, yielding DUET+. With the Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B backbone, DUET lifts the two-step quality of sCM close to the level of DMD while retaining nearly all of its structural diversity---about twice that of DMD---and DUET+ further improves overall quality while preserving this diversity advantage. Together, these results establish noise-level expert specialization as a simple, effective paradigm for reconciling diversity and quality in two-step video generation.

</details>


### [243] [EgoHieraLoc: A Cortically Inspired Hierarchical Segmentation-Guided Framework for Egocentric Visual Query Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09656)
*Yifei Cao,Guolong Wang,Mingliang Hou,Xiya Bu,Daming Liu,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoHieraLoc，一个统一框架用于解决第一人称视频中的视觉查询定位（VQL）任务，涵盖2D和3D定位。该框架模拟人类视觉的层次化处理过程，包含判别解析模块、查询感知模块和区域适应模块来逐步细化目标定位，并通过几何语义联合置信度（GSJC）机制实现可靠的3D定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉查询定位在物体边界模糊时具有挑战性，且全局上下文难以有效引导细粒度定位。作者从人类视觉的层次化处理过程（前景筛选、选择性注意、全局-局部反馈、多视角证据整合）获得灵感，旨在解决现有方法在边界模糊和全局上下文引导不足方面的局限。

Method: 提出EgoHieraLoc统一框架：1) 判别解析模块利用分割先验提取前景感知的查询表示；2) 查询感知模块通过判别相关滤波和可变形建模实现鲁棒目标定位；3) 区域适应模块将多尺度上下文反馈到局部区域以恢复精确目标边界。针对3D定位，提出几何语义联合置信度（GSJC），通过乘法耦合分割置信度与局部深度一致性、多视图反投影一致性和三角化基线质量，确保视角仅在语义和几何上均可信时才对3D估计做出贡献。

Result: 在VQL-2D和VQL-3D基准测试上均取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过模拟人类层次化视觉处理机制，EgoHieraLoc有效解决了边界模糊和全局上下文引导不足的问题，在2D和3D视觉查询定位任务中均达到最优效果，验证了该框架的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Visual query localization (VQL) aims to retrieve and re-localize a queried object in egocentric videos, yet remains challenging when object boundaries are ambiguous and global context cannot effectively guide fine-grained localization. Human vision handles such ambiguity through a hierarchical process: it rapidly screens foreground candidates, selectively attends to the target despite distractors, refines perception via feedback between global context and local detail, and, when a single view is unreliable, integrates evidence across viewpoints according to its credibility. Inspired by these competencies, we propose \textbf{EgoHieraLoc}, a unified framework for VQL-2D and VQL-3D. A Discriminative Parsing Module first extracts foreground-aware query representations using segmentation priors; a Query-Aware Module then performs robust target localization through discriminative correlation filtering with deformable modeling; and a Regional Adaptation Module feeds multi-scale context back into local regions to recover precise object boundaries. To extend this perceptual hierarchy to 3D localization, we introduce Geometric-Semantic Joint Confidence (GSJC), which multiplicatively couples segmentation confidence with local depth consistency, multi-view back-projection consistency, and triangulation-baseline quality, so that a viewpoint contributes to the 3D estimate only when it is credible both semantically and geometrically. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on both VQL-2D and -3D benchmarks.

</details>


### [244] [CIFA: Contextual-Intersectional Fairness Auditing for Hidden Subgroup Discovery in Face Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09669)
*Nazia Aslam,Khalid Adnan Alsayed,Thomas B. Moeslund,Kamal Nasrollahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CIFA框架，用于在计算机视觉模型中审计由人口统计属性与上下文属性（如光照、模糊、图像质量等）交互形成的隐藏子群性能退化问题，并在人脸性别分类任务上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机视觉公平性评估主要依赖聚合准确率和人口统计子群分析，但忽略了上下文因素（光照、模糊、图像质量、面部配饰等）与人口统计特征之间的交互作用，这些交互可能产生隐藏子群，虽然整体准确率和人口统计公平性看起来可接受，但性能却存在显著退化。

Method: 提出CIFA（Contextual-Intersectional Fairness Auditing Framework）框架，包含三个层次：人口统计审计、上下文审计和上下文-交叉审计，随后进行最差子群发现（worst-group discovery）来识别和排序最脆弱的属性组合。采用审计-缓解-再审计（audit-mitigate-reaudit）协议评估多种已有缓解策略的效果。

Result: 在ResNet-50和ViT-B/16两种架构上，使用FairFace、CelebA和UTKFace三个数据集进行性别分类评估。结果表明聚合准确率和仅人口统计的评估可能掩盖显著的上下文-交叉差异。已有缓解策略虽能减少部分最差子群差异，但没有任何单一策略能在不同数据集和架构上一致地消除这些差异。

Conclusion: 上下文-交叉审计应成为公平性评估的重要组成部分，该框架为在人脸分析系统中发现、优先排序和重新评估隐藏子群风险提供了可复现的方法。

Abstract: Fairness evaluation in computer vision commonly relies on aggregate accuracy and demographic subgroup analysis. However, visual models are also sensitive to contextual factors such as illumination, blur, image quality, facial accessories, and appearance attributes. These factors may interact with demographic characteristics, producing hidden subgroups in which performance degrades substantially despite strong aggregate accuracy and apparently acceptable demographic fairness. To address this, we propose the Contextual-Intersectional Fairness Auditing Framework (CIFA), a structured framework for identifying subgroup vulnerabilities arising from interactions between demographic and contextual attributes. CIFA performs demographic, contextual, and contextual-intersectional auditing, followed by worst-group discovery to identify and rank the most vulnerable attribute combinations. We evaluate CIFA on gender classification using ResNet-50 \cite{he2016deep} and ViT-B/16 \cite{dosovitskiy2020image} across FairFace \cite{Karkkainen2021}, CelebA \cite{Liu2015}, and UTKFace \cite{Zhang2017}. Our results show that aggregate accuracy and demographic-only evaluation can mask substantial contextual-intersectional disparities. We further assess several established mitigation strategies through an audit--mitigate--reaudit protocol and find that, although some worst-group disparities are reduced, no single strategy consistently eliminates them across datasets and architectures. These findings establish contextual-intersectional auditing as an important component of fairness evaluation and provide a reproducible framework for discovering, prioritizing, and reassessing hidden subgroup risks in face analysis systems.

</details>


### [245] [MPISuperRes-PnP: A Super-Resolution Zero-Shot Plug-and-Play Reconstruction Algorithm for Magnetic Particle Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09672)
*Vladyslav Gapyak,Thomas März,Andreas Weinmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于能量最小化的磁性粒子成像（MPI）超分辨率方法，采用即插即用（plug-and-play）框架，将预训练的深度学习高斯去噪器以零样本方式集成到超分辨率重建中，避免了训练数据的需求，并在合成数据和真实数据上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPI重建即使使用最先进的基于测量的方法，其空间网格仍然非常粗糙，因此超分辨率技术至关重要。现有的SR方法包括系统矩阵上采样或重建插值，但均存在局限性；同时深度学习需要大量MPI训练数据，而这类数据目前稀缺。

Method: 将超分辨率通过能量最小化公式整合到重建任务中，采用即插即用方法推导出分裂方案。其中高斯去噪任务由预训练的学习型高斯去噪器以零样本方式处理，无需训练即可利用深度学习的优势。超参数通过扩展参数搜索进行选择。

Result: 该方法在合成数据和真实数据（MPIData: EquilibriumModelWithAnisotropy和2D-OpenMPI Data）上均验证了适用性。去噪器表现保守，未观察到幻觉伪影。

Conclusion: 该方法不需要目前稀缺的MPI训练数据，去噪器行为保守稳健，超分辨率方法具有通用性，可应用于未来涉及不同正则化器或不同成像任务的MPI场景。

Abstract: Magnetic Particle Imaging (MPI) is an emerging medical imaging modality. MPI is based on the non-linear response of magnetic nanoparticles to an applied magnetic field and avoids ionizing radiation. The measured signal is the voltage induced in receive coils by the particles' response. Reconstructing the particle concentration from the signal constitutes the imaging task. Even using state-of-the-art measurement-based reconstruction, the associated spatial grid is very coarse, hence super-resolution (SR) techniques are important. In this work, we propose an approach for SR in MPI inspired by energy minimization. Different methods have been proposed for SR in MPI, ranging from upscaling of the associated system matrix to interpolation of the reconstruction. Here we incorporate SR into the reconstruction task via an energy minimization formulation. Following the plug-and-play approach to energy minimization we derive a splitting scheme and a SR method for MPI where the arising Gaussian denoising task is treated with a pre-trained learned Gaussian denoiser in a zero-shot fashion. This way, we incorporate benefits of deep learning without training and avoid the need of training data. Further, we provide a quantitative and qualitative evaluation of the proposed method. Hyper-parameter are selected via an extended parameter search. The found parameters are applied for reconstruction on real data. We show the applicability of our method on synthetic and on real data (MPIData: EquilibriumModelWithAnisotropy and 2D-OpenMPI Data). The proposed method employs a deep-learning denoiser without training -- thus it does not require presently scarcely available MPI training data. The denoiser behaves conservatively, i.e., no hallucination artifacts were observed. The SR approach is generic such that it can be applied in future MPI contexts involving different regularizers or different imaging tasks.

</details>


### [246] [Thinking With Tools, Not With Pixels: Tool Calls as Text Scaffolds for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09682)
*Jiahao Shao,Yuanbo Yang,Yiyi Liao,Yujun Shen,Ceyuan Yang,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TextCall方法，通过保留工具调用的文本脚手架（工具名、坐标、目标描述）但替换返回图像为占位符，证明在当前的"用图像思考"视觉语言模型中，真正起作用的信号是调用工具时产生的结构化文本，而返回的图像像素只是冗余载体。TextCall在三项研究中验证了返回像素的非必要性、文本脚手架的有效性以及各成分的贡献，在保持精度的同时将延迟降低29-46%并消除工具执行API调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强的视觉语言模型（VLM）声称通过调用裁剪、缩放或代码工具并从返回的像素中推理来"思考图像"，但近年来的盲测、增益分解和注意力分析发现返回图像贡献甚微。作者想探究：如果像素不携带增益，那么真正的信息载体是什么？

Method: 作者提出工具调用脚手架假说（Tool-Call Scaffold Hypothesis）：负载信号的来源是工具调用时发出的结构化文本，包括工具名、坐标、目标描述和意图。为验证这一假说，他们设计了TextCall方法——保留脚手架文本但将返回图像替换为文本占位符"[Image output skipped]"。通过三项研究进行验证：(i)跨LoRA、全参数微调和强化学习设置，比较TextCall与完整"用图像思考"的效果；(ii)在匹配的训练查询上，比较仅脚手架输入与返回图像输入的准确性；(iii)将脚手架分解为推理文本和空间代码，分析各成分的贡献。

Result: 三项研究均支持该假说：(i)在LoRA、全参数微调和RL下，TextCall匹配或超越完整思考图像的效果；在RL下TextCall保持了工具使用行为，而看到返回图像反而导致模型停止调用工具并直接回答；(ii)在匹配查询上，仅脚手架输入与返回图像输入的准确性相当；(iii)推理文本和空间代码两个成分都有贡献，但主导成分因任务而异。TextCall在保持精度的同时将延迟降低29-46%，并消除了工具执行API调用。

Conclusion: 当前"用图像思考"的分布中，活跃信号是工具调用时发出的结构化文本，返回图像是冗余载体。但作者明确指出该结论仅限于当前的思考图像基准测试，构建像素真正起关键作用的任务仍是开放的研究方向。

Abstract: Tool-augmented vision-language models increasingly "think with images": they call crop, zoom, or code tools and reason over the returned pixels. However, recent work using blind tests, gain decompositions, and attention analyses has shown that returned images contribute little, raising the question: if pixels do not carry the gain, what does? We hypothesize that the load-bearing signal is the structured text emitted before any returned pixel arrives: tool name, coordinates, target description, and intent. This textual scaffold encodes where to look and what to find. We introduce TextCall (call-but-no-return) to test this: it keeps the scaffold but replaces returned images with the text placeholder [Image output skipped]. Three studies support the hypothesis. (i) Non-necessity of returned pixels: across LoRA, full fine-tuning, and RL, TextCall matches or exceeds full thinking-with-images; under RL it preserves tool use at the reported checkpoint, avoiding the failure mode where, under matched settings, seeing the returned image causes the model to stop calling tools and answer directly. (ii) Sufficiency of the scaffold: on matched training queries, scaffold-only input yields equivalent accuracy to returned-image input. (iii) Component specificity: decomposing the scaffold into reasoning text and spatial code shows both components contribute, with the dominant one varying by task. Together these results support the Tool-Call Scaffold Hypothesis: in current thinking-with-images distributions, the active signal is the structured text emitted at tool-call time; the returned image is a redundant carrier. TextCall preserves accuracy while reducing latency by 29-46% and eliminating tool-execution API calls. Our claims hold for current thinking-with-images benchmarks; constructing tasks where pixels are genuinely load-bearing remains an open direction.

</details>


### [247] [Diffuse the object, keep its label: curating detector training data from a few unlabeled photographs via VLM-built 3D vegetation scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09691)
*Mario Malizia,Marnix Enting,Rob Haelterman,Ken Hasselmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无监督站点自适应方法：利用少量部署站点照片，通过视觉-语言模型生成粗略3D植被场景，放入3D物体网格以自动获得标注（边界框、分割掩码、遮挡信息），再用扩散模型重贴纹理合成训练图像，使检测器在该站点无需人工标注即可训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植被中隐藏的小物体标注图像稀缺，且基于其他站点标注训练的检测器在不同地点泛化性能差，传统的跨站点复用标签方式效果不佳。

Method: 1) 用视觉-语言模型从单张照片生成粗略3D植被场景；2) 将3D物体网格放入场景，从场景几何直接获得标注；3) 用轻量适配器（在照片上微调）控制扩散过程对渲染图进行重贴纹理；4) 采用分级掩码锁定（graded mask-lock）控制扩散对物体本身的影响程度。

Result: 在一个人道主义排雷基准上，该方法训练的检测器在多个随机种子下达到或超过使用更大规模真实跨站点标注数据训练的检测器；轻度扩散物体可提升少数类召回率，而完全保护像素或完全放开扩散效果均不佳。

Conclusion: 从少量部署照片合成标注训练图像可实现有效的无监督站点自适应，且分级掩码锁定是最关键的调优选择；增益对照片和裁剪预算不敏感，域内准确率无法预测跨站点性能。

Abstract: Labeled images of small objects hidden in vegetation are scarce, and detectors trained on them generalize poorly across sites. Rather than reusing labels collected at another site, we synthesize labeled training images from a handful of unlabeled photographs of the deployment site itself. A vision--language model generates a coarse 3D vegetation scene from one photograph; placing 3D object meshes in the scene yields bounding boxes, segmentation masks, and per-instance occlusion directly from the scene geometry, without manual annotation. A lightweight adapter fine-tuned on the photographs conditions a diffusion pass that re-textures the renders, and a graded mask-lock sets how much diffusion may touch the object itself. In our runs this grade was the most influential curation choice: lightly diffusing the object improves minority-class recall over fully protecting its pixels, while unrestricted diffusion dissolves it. Trained on these images, a standard detector matched or exceeded its counterpart trained on a larger labeled dataset of real images from a different site, consistently across seeds on a humanitarian-demining benchmark; the comparison is thus unsupervised site adaptation from a handful of photographs against conventional cross-site label reuse. In our ablations the gains were largely insensitive to the photograph and crop budgets, and in-domain accuracy did not predict cross-site performance.

</details>


### [248] [LookAgain: Closed-Loop GUI Grounding with Visually Grounded Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09723)
*Renshan Zhang,Haoyang Meng,Yixiao He,Rui Shao,April Hua Liu,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LookAgain，一个闭环GUI grounding模型，通过"定位-再看-修正"的多轮反思机制，将预测坐标视为可检验的假设，在预测后利用视觉证据进行自我修正，显著提升小目标、密集控件和分布外界面的定位准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI grounding方法虽然在标准基准上表现出高单次准确率，但在小目标、密集控件和分布外界面场景下性能急剧下降。作者将这一差距归因于现有方法的范式性局限：没有把产生的坐标视为需要基于新视觉证据进行反思和修正的假设，表现为三个耦合问题——缺乏事后反思、视觉证据与预测解耦、迭代优化的是视图而非预测本身。

Method: LookAgain将grounding重构为多轮"预测-再看-修正"的闭环过程，包含两个原语：1) "locate"：提出坐标假设，在图像上渲染标记并附加预测区域的局部patch，将下一步推理锚定到前一次预测作为空间先验；2) "confirm"：接受或拒绝假设并终止流程。训练采用两个阶段：先通过SFT在构造的反思轨迹上冷启动，再用GRPO以终端grounding正确性作为唯一奖励进行强化学习。

Result: 大量实验表明LookAgain在拒绝感知和通用GUI grounding基准上均持续提升性能，达到最先进水平。全面的消融实验进一步验证了所提框架各组成部分的有效性。

Conclusion: 将预测视为可反思和修正的假设而非最终答案，通过闭环的视觉反思机制能够突破现有GUI grounder在困难场景下的性能瓶颈，为该领域提供了新的范式方向。

Abstract: Recent graphical user interface (GUI) grounders have significantly advanced single-shot accuracy on standard benchmarks, yet their performance degrades sharply on small targets, densely packed controls and out-of-distribution interfaces. We attribute this gap to a paradigmatic limitation shared by existing approaches: none of them treats a produced coordinate as a hypothesis to be reflected upon and revised under new visual evidence. This manifests as three coupled issues: 1) Lack of post-hoc reflection. The prediction is frozen at the moment of emission, leaving no internal mechanism to challenge or refine it. 2) Visual evidence decoupled from the prediction. The auxiliary visual evidence is gathered to support the upcoming coordinate rather than to scrutinise the one already committed to. 3) Refinement over views, not over predictions. The iterative zoom-in refines the inspected region instead of inheriting a previous coordinate as a spatial prior to be corrected. In this paper, we propose LookAgain, a closed-loop GUI grounder driven by post-prediction visual reflection. LookAgain reformulates grounding as a multi-turn predict-look-again-refine process with two primitives: "locate" posts a coordinate hypothesis, renders a marker on the image and appends a local patch of the predicted region. It anchors the next reasoning step to the previous prediction as a spatial prior; "confirm" accepts or reject the hypothesis and terminates the procedure. We train the LookAgain grounder with SFT on constructed reflective trajectories as a cold start, followed by GRPO with terminal grounding correctness as the sole reward. Extensive experiments show that LookAgain consistently improves performance on both refusal-aware and general GUI grounding benchmarks, achieving state-of-the-art results. Comprehensive ablations further verify the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [249] [World Tokens: Enhancing Embodied Policies with Training-Time World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09730)
*Qu Tang,Benhui Zhuang,Bo Yuan,Xue Yu,Longteng Guo,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出World Tokens架构，通过世界适配器将VLM特征转化为一组世界token，在训练时用未来视频去噪器增强动作策略，部署时移除世界模型分支，实现高效的动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽擅长闭环控制，但不显式建模物理场景演化；而世界动作模型（WAMs）虽能捕捉时空演化，但在控制回路中保留视频生成或大型视频骨干会大幅增加推理成本。

Method: World Tokens架构由世界适配器桥接视觉语言理解、世界动力学建模和动作生成。世界适配器将VLM特征转换为固定数量的世界token，这些token同时条件化联合微调的未来视频去噪器，并作为动作专家的唯一视觉语言上下文。共享条件化使视频去噪梯度直接塑造动作预测表征，独占路由防止策略绕过该表征。部署时移除世界模型分支，仅保留VLM、世界适配器和动作专家。

Result: 在2B骨干和无动作预训练条件下，World Tokens在LIBERO上极具竞争力，在SIMPLER上取得最佳平均成绩，显著提升真实世界R1 Pro成功率，且动作块生成速度达到VLA级别延迟。

Conclusion: 世界建模在训练时能够有效增强动作策略的性能，同时通过训练/部署分离的设计在推理阶段保持高效，无需在线视频模型推理。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are a widely adopted paradigm for embodied policies. They excel at efficient closed-loop control but do not explicitly model how physical scenes evolve as a task unfolds. Recently emerging world-action models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video world models to capture spatiotemporal evolution, yet retaining future generation or a large video backbone in the control loop substantially increases inference cost. We introduce World Tokens, an embodied policy architecture built around a World Adapter that bridges visual-language understanding, world-dynamics modeling, and action generation. It uses world modeling during training to enhance the action policy while preserving efficient deployment. Specifically, the World Adapter transforms VLM features into a fixed set of world tokens, which condition a jointly fine-tuned future-video denoiser and simultaneously serve as the action expert's sole visual-language context. This shared conditioning allows gradients from future-video denoising to directly shape the representation used for action prediction, while exclusive routing prevents the policy from bypassing that representation. At deployment, the world-model branch is removed, leaving only the VLM, World Adapter, and action expert, with no online video-model inference. With a 2B backbone and no embodied action pretraining, World Tokens is highly competitive on LIBERO, attains the best reported averages on SIMPLER, substantially improves real-world R1 Pro success over a matched action-only baseline, and generates each action chunk at VLA-level latency.

</details>


### [250] [HandSplatter: Automated Digital Goniometry from Neural Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09735)
*Emmett Chen,Neal Chen,Xiang Li,Quanzheng Li,Siyeop Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经渲染的3D手部关节定位与姿态估计新流程，结合2D特征提取与视图合成提升精度，并引入离散密度爬山算法校正投影标志点，为手部手指屈伸活动度提供客观、准确的自动化测量工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手指疾病是肌肉骨骼残疾的主要因素，需要精确量化关节运动的方法。现有标准工具量角器依赖人工操作，效率低且评分者间信度不一致；数字替代方案虽存在但缺乏临床所需的准确性。

Method: 提出一种结合神经渲染的3D手部关节定位与姿态估计新流程，融合2D特征提取与视图合成以提高精度；同时引入离散密度爬山算法，用于在3D空间中有效校正投影后的标志点。

Result: 该系统克服了人工测量的低效率和现有软件的精度不足问题，显著提升了关节测量的准确性和临床可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法为客观功能评估提供了稳健的工具，可作为传统量角器和现有数字方法的可靠替代方案。

Abstract: Hand and finger disorders are leading contributors to musculoskeletal disability, creating a clinical need for precise methods to quantify joint motion. Range of motion (ROM) serves as the metric for diagnosis, rehabilitation monitoring, and evaluating surgical outcomes. Currently, the goniometer is the standard tool for assessing finger flexion and extension. However, manual goniometry is labor-intensive and suffers from inconsistent inter-rater reliability due to variations in examiner technique. While digital alternatives exist, current software-based approaches often lack the necessary accuracy for clinical usage. To address these limitations, we present a novel pipeline for 3-D hand joint location and pose estimation using neural rendering. Unlike previous methods, our approach combines 2-D feature extraction with view synthesis to significantly improve accuracy and clinical viability. Furthermore, we introduce a discrete density hill climbing algorithm that facilitates the meaningful correction of projected landmarks in 3-D space. This system overcomes the inefficiencies of manual measurement and the inaccuracies of existing software, providing a robust tool for objective functional assessment.

</details>


### [251] [Disentangling Co-Occurring Retinal Pathologies with Saliency-Guided Sparse Expert Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09752)
*Nagur Shareef Shaik,Jeongwoo Park,Yeong-Jin Kim,Jaeuk Jung,Hyunjung Oh,Dong Hye Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于稀疏条件计算的视网膜眼底图像多疾病分类架构，通过引导上下文门控注意力前端与稀疏路由的混合专家模块相结合，实现了可解释的疾病解耦分析，在五类基准上取得0.912宏AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜眼底图像常存在多种共病情况，但标准深度学习分类器对所有图像采用静态且相同的计算，无法根据疾病分布自适应调整，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 提出新型架构，包括两个核心组件：(1) 引导上下文门控(GCG)空间注意力前端；(2) 在特征token上运行的稀疏路由混合专家(MoE)模块。通过稀疏条件计算实现路由的可解释数据驱动分解。

Result: 在五类、按患者分离的五折交叉验证基准上，模型达到0.912±0.008宏AUC和0.653±0.014宏F1。专家分配与疾病显著相关(p<0.001)，健康状态与形态学上独特的病理（如ERM、AMD）被隔离到专属专家。Grad-CAM++和后MoE t-SNE可视化证实专家路由与局部病灶对齐，共病案例在几何上映射于其组成簇之间。

Conclusion: 稀疏MoE可作为多疾病视网膜筛查的可解释方法，既能通过条件计算提升分类性能，又能通过数据驱动的专家分配揭示疾病结构与共病关系。

Abstract: Retinal fundus images frequently exhibit multiple co-occurring pathologies, yet standard deep learning classifiers apply static, identical computation to every image regardless of the underlying disease distribution. We propose a novel architecture that resolves this via sparse conditional computation, pairing a Guided Context Gating (GCG) spatial attention front-end with a sparsely-routed Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) block operating over feature tokens. Crucially, this routing yields an interpretable, data-driven decomposition. Expert allocation is significantly disease-dependent (p < 0.001), with the healthy Normal state and morphologically distinct pathologies (e.g., ERM, AMD) isolating to dedicated experts. On a five-class, patient-disjoint 5-fold cross-validation benchmark, our model achieves 0.912 +/- 0.008 macro AUC and 0.653 +/- 0.014 macro F1. Furthermore, Grad-CAM++ and post-MoE t-SNE visualizations confirm that expert routing aligns with localized lesions and geometrically maps co-occurring cases between their constituent clusters, positioning sparse MoE as an interpretable approach to multi-disease retinal screening.

</details>


### [252] [C$^2$A: Coupling Spatial Evidence with Clinical Priors via Co-occurrence Aware Class Attention for Multi-Label Chest X-Ray Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09774)
*Akash Gogineni,Nagur Shareef Shaik,Aasrith Mandava,Adnan Masood,Dong Hye Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出C²A（共现感知类别注意力）分类头，通过将池化操作转化为学习到的每类空间注意力图的期望，获得每个疾病的局部描述符，并利用从经验标签共现中热启动的可学习图来耦合这些描述符。在CheXpert数据集上达到0.895的宏平均AUROC，超过先进基线，且增益集中在高共现、空间证据模糊的类别上，计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部病理很少单独出现，但标准多标签分类器依赖共享的全局描述符，忽略了病变的位置信息及其共现关系，限制了分类性能。

Method: C²A首先将池化视为对学习到的每类空间注意力图的期望，为每个疾病生成局部描述符；随后通过从经验标签共现热启动的可学习图来耦合这些描述符，使用单次残差消息传递步骤在相关发现间共享证据，共现通过显式双线性交互进入每个logit。

Result: 在CheXpert数据集上，C²A达到0.895的宏平均AUROC，优于先进的情境门控基线。增益集中在高共现且空间证据模糊的类别上（如肺不张相对GCG提升+1.5），同时仅增加一个线性投影和一个C×C边矩阵的开销，可忽略不计。

Conclusion: C²A证明了将空间证据定位与临床共现先验显式耦合能够有效提升胸部X光多标签分类性能，尤其在处理空间证据模糊的高共现疾病时表现突出，且开销极小，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Thoracic pathologies rarely occur in isolation, yet standard multi-label classifiers rely on shared global descriptors, discarding \emph{where} findings lie and \emph{how} they co-occur. We propose \textbf{C$\mathbf{^2}$A} (Co-occurrence Aware Class Attention), a classification head that explicitly couples spatial evidence with clinical priors. First, C$^2$A casts pooling as an expectation over learned per-class spatial attention maps, yielding localized descriptors for each disease. Second, it couples these descriptors via a learnable graph warm-started from empirical label co-occurrence. A single residual message-passing step shares evidence among related findings, proving to be a bounded perturbation of the identity where co-occurrence enters each logit through an explicit bilinear interaction. On CheXpert, C$^2$A achieves a superior $0.895$ macro-mean AUROC, outperforming advanced context-gating baselines. Crucially, gains concentrate on highly co-occurrent classes with ambiguous spatial evidence (rescuing Atelectasis by $+1.5$ over GCG), demonstrating the prior's regularizing effect with a negligible overhead of one linear projection and a $C\!\times\!C$ edge matrix.

</details>


### [253] [NTIRE 2026 Low-light Enhancement: Twilight Cowboy Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09782)
*Aleksei Khalin,Egor Ershov,Artyom Panshin,Sergey Korchagin,Georgiy Lobarev,Arseniy Terekhin,Sofiia Dorogova,Amir Shamsutdinov,Yasin Mamedov,Bakhtiyar Khalfin,Bogdan Sheludko,Emil Zilyaev,Nikola Banić,Georgy Perevozchikov,Radu Timofte,Shuai Liu,Yuqian Zhang,Lize Zhang,Yibin Huang,Chaoyu Feng,Luyang Wang,Xiaotao Wang,Dongqing Zou,Lei Lei,Tianli Liu,Dejun Hao,Chunxia Lei,Furkan Kınlı,Andrei Mironov,Alexander Dikov,Aleksei Sadokhin,Vladimir Zvorygin,Constantine Habarlak,Shuwei Yue,Egor Mirantsov,Daniil Okunev,Dmitry Arkhipov,Aleksandr Yugay,Anas M. Ali,Bilel Benjdira,Wadii Boulila,Wei Zhou,Linfeng Li,Lingdong Kong,Jiachen Tu,Guoyi Xu,Yaoxin Jiang,Jiajia Liu,Yaokun Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是对NTIRE 2026低光增强挑战赛（Twilight Cowboy）的综述报告，该挑战赛旨在将低光条件下拍摄的多张未对齐智能手机raw域图像合成为一张干净图像，同时解决低光摄影中的视觉退化（噪声、混合光照）和手部运动引起的几何不一致问题。比赛收集了585个真实场景的数据集，采用三阶段评估协议，最终10支团队超越了基线，在PSNR上最高提升+6.49 dB，在SSIM上最高提升+0.0101，刷新了突发低光图像增强的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光摄影中用户常面临多重挑战：高噪声、混合场景光照等视觉退化问题，以及手持拍摄多帧时的运动导致的几何不一致问题。现有方法通常分别处理这些问题，缺乏一个同时解决两者的统一框架和真实场景基准数据集。

Method: 该挑战赛构建了一个包含585个真实世界场景的挑战性数据集（覆盖室内低光和室外夜间场景），采用三阶段评估协议：阶段一和阶段二通过CodaBench平台自动验证，最终阶段在私有测试集上进行盲评以确定排名。

Result: 10支参赛团队超越了预设基线，最高在PSNR上提升+6.49 dB，SSIM上提升+0.0101，建立了突发低光图像增强的新最优性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在低光场景中处理真实噪声、运动和不均匀照明的技术已取得显著进展，该挑战赛推动了低光和夜间计算摄影领域的研究发展，相关结果、排行榜和额外信息已公开。

Abstract: This paper presents a review of the NTIRE 2026 Low-light Enhancement: Twilight Cowboy Challenge. The objective of the competition was to merge a set of misaligned smartphone images in the raw domain, captured in low-light conditions, into a single, clean image. Introduced setup simultaneously addresses two problems of low-light photography: visual degradations such as high noise and mixed scene illuminants, and the geometric inconsistencies caused by hand movement during multi-frame capture.
