To advance research in low-light and nighttime computational photography, a challenging dataset was collected comprising 585 real-world scenes, spanning indoor low-light and outdoor nighttime conditions, for training and benchmarking participant solutions. The competition employed a three-stage evaluation protocol: automatic validation via the CodaBench platform in stages one and two, followed by blind assessment on a private test set for the final ranking. Ten teams surpassed the established baseline, achieving improvements of up to +6.49 dB in PSNR and +0.0101 in SSIM, thereby establishing new state-of-the-art performance for burst-based low-light image enhancement. These results demonstrate significant progress in handling real-world noise, motion, and illumination variability in the low-light setting. Comprehensive results, leaderboards, and additional information are publicly available at https://nightimaging.org.

</details>


### [254] [ADOPD: Reference-Privileged On-Policy Distillation for MLLM-Based Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09789)
*Jingtai He,Shiyuan Meng,Wenchao Meng,Qinmin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ADOPD框架，通过参考特权的在线策略蒸馏，将多模态大语言模型在工业异常检测中依赖推理时参考比较的能力内化到模型参数中。在MMAD基准上零样本推理达到77.31%平均准确率，比Qwen3-VL-4B骨干提升6.14个百分点，甚至超过其一回合（one-shot）设置的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在工业异常检测（IAD）中可通过推理时比较查询图像与参考图像来提升识别精度，但这种优势依赖额外的检索和处理开销。作者希望探究能否将参考比较的收益内化到模型参数中，使查询图像单独作为输入时也能获得类似的性能提升。

Method: ADOPD采用参考特权的在线策略蒸馏框架。训练时，参考感知的教师模型监督仅使用查询图像的学生模型。具体包含两个关键机制：(1) 教师在匹配和不匹配的参考条件下评估学生生成的rollouts，利用匹配参考下的教师-学生对数比（log-ratio）定义token级学习方向；(2) 两种参考视图之间的似然差距用于估计参考特定支持度，并校准序列级权重。

Result: ADOPD在MMAD基准上零样本推理达到77.31%的平均准确率，比Qwen3-VL-4B骨干模型提升6.14个百分点，且比该模型的一回合（one-shot）设置高出2.64个百分点。实验表明ADOPD从参考比较中学习到了细粒度的异常检测策略。

Conclusion: ADOPD成功证明参考比较的收益可以有效地内化到模型参数中，在不牺牲推理效率的前提下实现了超越one-shot设置的性能，为工业异常检测中的零样本推理提供了新的蒸馏范式。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection (IAD) requires identifying fine-grained deviations from normal visual patterns. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can improve recognition accuracy by comparing query images with references at inference time, but these benefits rely on additional retrieval and processing. We investigate whether the benefits of reference comparison can instead be internalized in the model parameters. Access to references during training allows a reference-aware teacher to supervise a query-only student. However, the teacher may favor plausible responses based on query cues or language priors rather than valid visual information. We propose ADOPD, a reference-privileged on-policy distillation framework. The teacher evaluates student-generated rollouts under matched and mismatched references. The matched-reference teacher-to-student log-ratio defines the token-level learning direction, specifying what the student should learn. The likelihood gap between the two reference views estimates reference-specific support and calibrates the sequence-level weight. ADOPD achieves 77.31% average accuracy on the MMAD benchmark under zero-shot inference, improving the Qwen3-VL-4B backbone by 6.14 points and outperforming its one-shot setting by 2.64 points. Experiments show that ADOPD learns a fine-grained anomaly inspection strategy from reference comparison. The project will be available at https://github.com/withTai/ADOPD.

</details>


### [255] [Modern Backbones Improve Multi-task DETR for Mammography Classification and Lesion Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09801)
*Dinh Tan Nguyen,Quang-Hien Kha,Le-Hoang Nguyen,Minh-Toan Dinh,Xuan-Huy Nguyen,Dac Phu Ho,Cao Truong Tran,Sai Ho Ling,Lan T Ho-Pham,Liem Pham,Nguyen Quoc Khanh Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多任务DETR框架在乳腺X线摄影中的联合图像级恶性预测和病灶定位，比较了多种现代骨干网络，发现ConvNeXtV2和DINOv3分别在不同数据集上表现最佳，ConvNeXtV2在OPTIMAM达到97.96% AUC，DINOv3在SGM1k达到90.97% AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合进行考试级预测和候选区域定位可能提升AI在乳腺X线摄影中的实用性，因此需要研究多任务框架下的方法，并探究不同骨干网络对性能的影响。

Method: 采用多任务DETR框架，通过共享表示同时支持图像级恶性预测和病灶定位，在OPTIMAM和活检确诊的SGM1k两个数据集上评估现代骨干网络（ConvNeXtV2、DINOv3、MambaVision等）与旧式ResNet风格特征的表现。

Result: 在OPTIMAM上，ConvNeXtV2表现最佳，达到97.96% AUC、99.89%灵敏度、25.08% mAP@.5、74.38% recall@.25；在SGM1k上，DINOv3表现最强，达到90.97% AUC、86.28%灵敏度、82.00%特异性、27.04% mAP@.5、77.32% recall@.25。现代骨干普遍优于ResNet风格特征，MambaVision表现相对较弱。

Conclusion: 骨干网络质量是多任务乳腺X线摄影中的关键因素，ConvNeXtV2被证明是该框架下特别强大且适配良好的CNN骨干网络。

Abstract: Joint exam-level prediction and candidate-region localization may improve the usefulness of AI support in mammography. We study this setting using a multi-task DETR framework, where shared representations support both image-level malignancy prediction and lesion localization, and evaluate its performance on OPTIMAM and a biopsy-confirmed SGM1k cohort. Across both datasets, modern backbones consistently outperformed older ResNet-style features, with ConvNeXtV2 and DINOv3 giving the strongest overall results, whereas MambaVision was less competitive. On OPTIMAM, ConvNeXtV2 achieved the best overall performance, reaching 97.96% AUC, 99.89% sensitivity, 25.08% mAP@.5, and 74.38% recall@.25. On SGM1k, DINOv3 gave the strongest overall results, with 90.97% AUC, 86.28% sensitivity, 82.00% specificity, 27.04% mAP@.5, and 77.32% recall@.25. These findings suggest that backbone quality is a critical factor in effective multi-task mammography, with ConvNeXtV2 emerging as a particularly strong and well-matched CNN backbone for mammography in this framework.

</details>


### [256] [MedPixel: A Unified Pixel-Language Model for Medical Reasoning and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09818)
*Haoyu Yang,Meixing Shi,Zengjie Chen,Haoran Sun,Haitao Leng,Xiaoming Shi,Yuxiang Cai,Yankai Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedPixel是一个统一的医学像素-语言模型，通过共享的语言-掩码接口连接临床语言理解与像素级分割，并引入MedPLG-440K数据集（约44万个像素-语言任务样本）和像素级偏好优化实现多任务联合训练，支持显式定位、隐式推理、空间交互、可解释分割和医学VQA等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉-语言模型缺乏精确的像素级定位能力，而医学分割模型又依赖明确的目标类别或精确的空间提示。根本原因在于监督信号不匹配：分割数据集有精确掩码但缺乏语言标注，视觉-语言数据集又缺少稠密的空间标注，导致两类模型之间存在难以弥合的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 提出MedPixel统一模型，构建共享的语言-掩码接口；2) 提出MedPLG-440K数据集，通过临床动机驱动的合成流程生成约44万个像素-语言任务样本，无需外部LLM标注；3) 采用联合多任务监督微调训练；4) 引入像素级偏好优化（Pixel-Level Preference Optimization），利用真实掩码作为离线验证器，根据掩码质量推导响应偏好。

Result: MedPixel在像素级预测和响应生成方面均取得强性能，在外部定位基准上实现了有效的零样本迁移，并且对不完美的空间提示具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过统一语言与掩码接口以及创新的数据合成和偏好优化策略，MedPixel有效弥合了医学语言理解与像素级定位之间的鸿沟，为医学图像理解提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable medical image understanding requires models to connect clinical language and visual reasoning with pixel-level grounding. Yet medical vision-language models often lack precise localization, whereas medical segmenters typically rely on explicit target categories or precise spatial prompts. This divide is reinforced by a supervision mismatch: segmentation datasets provide precise masks but little language supervision, whereas medical vision-language data rarely pair language with dense spatial annotations. To address this gap, we present MedPixel, a unified medical pixel-language model built around a shared language--mask interface. To provide scalable supervision, we introduce MedPLG-440K, comprising approximately 440K pixel-language task samples constructed through a clinically motivated synthesis process without external LLM annotation. MedPixel is trained with joint multi-task supervised fine-tuning followed by Pixel-Level Preference Optimization, which uses ground-truth masks as offline verifiers to derive response preferences from mask quality. MedPixel supports a broad spectrum of tasks spanning explicit grounding, implicit reasoning, spatial interaction, grounded explanation, and medical VQA. Across this task spectrum, MedPixel achieves strong performance in both pixel-level prediction and response generation, together with effective zero-shot transfer to external grounding benchmarks and robustness to imperfect spatial prompts. Code and model checkpoints will be released at https://github.com/yhy-whu/Medpixel.

</details>


### [257] [From Diagnosis to Correction: Benchmarking and Improving Real-World Table Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09842)
*Jutao Xiao,Yuan Qu,Dongsheng Ma,Fan Wu,Tianyao He,Weihong Li,Jie Yang,Yu Qiao,Bin Wang,Conghui He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TableParseMap——一个包含916张真实复杂表格的诊断基准，并设计了DEC（分解-增强-修正）智能体框架，通过视觉一致性引导改进冻结表格解析器，无需重新训练即可提升平均1.57点TEDS分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析器在公开基准（如OmniDocBench）上TEDS分数超过93，但在真实复杂表格上仍存在持续性失效。聚合基准分数掩盖了模型在复杂场景下的显著弱点，亟需一个能量化这种性能差距的诊断基准。

Method: 本文提出DEC框架，由通用视觉语言模型（VLM）作为控制器，包含三个模块：Decompose沿结构感知边界分割大表格；Enhance暴露弱视觉证据并重新解析变换视图；Correct诊断并修复残留错误。此外设计了VC-Gate选择性地触发干预，VC-Ranker在无真实HTML的情况下验证候选更新并支持回滚。同时通过离线指标和跨模型共识从4556个候选表格中筛选出1977个构成的Consensus-Hard Set。

Result: 最强解析器在TableParseMap上仅达到85.03 TEDS。DEC在三个冻结解析器上平均提升1.57点TEDS；在TableParseMap上总体提升1.89点，结构性错误提升2.62点，大表格提升5.66点。

Conclusion: 聚合基准分数确实掩盖了复杂表格上的系统性缺陷，而无需重新训练、仅通过视觉一致性引导的智能体干预即可有效改进冻结表格解析器的性能。

Abstract: Recent document parsers achieve table TEDS scores above 93 on OmniDocBench v1.6, yet community feedback and our audit reveal persistent failures on complex real-world tables. To quantify this gap, we introduce TableParseMap, a diagnostic benchmark of 916 real-world tables organized into five challenging scenarios and nine failure types. The strongest evaluated parser achieves only 85.03 TEDS, showing that aggregate benchmark scores conceal substantial weaknesses. Our analysis attributes these failures to three complementary limitations: large tables exceed the reliable processing scale of a single pass, weak or ambiguous visual cues hinder structure perception, and the reconstructed table may remain visually inconsistent with the image. We therefore propose DEC (Decompose--Enhance--Correct), a visual-consistency-guided agentic framework that improves frozen table parsers without retraining. DEC uses a general VLM as the controller: Decompose partitions large tables along structure-aware boundaries, Enhance exposes weak visual evidence and reparses transformed views, and Correct diagnoses and repairs residual errors. A Visual Consistency Gate (VC-Gate) selectively triggers intervention, while a Visual Consistency Ranker (VC-Ranker) verifies candidate updates and supports rollback without ground-truth HTML at inference time. We further derive a 1,977-table Consensus-Hard Set from 4,556 candidates through offline metrics and cross-model consensus. Across three frozen parsers, DEC improves TEDS by 1.57 points on average; on TableParseMap, gains reach 1.89 points overall, 2.62 on structural errors, and 5.66 on large tables.

</details>


### [258] [Sci-VBench: Evaluating Knowledge- and Reasoning-Intensive Video Generation in Science Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09873)
*Diandian Zhang,Tingyu Song,Lin Fu,Zheyuan Yang,Yilun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Sci-VBench，一个面向科学领域的知识密集型和推理密集型视频生成基准，包含1,253个专家标注示例，覆盖四大核心学科的60个主题，并建立基于评分规则的评估协议。通过评估16个前沿模型发现，尽管视觉质量分数趋同，但科学因果正确性表现差异显著，且开源与闭源模型差距明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成基准主要关注表面视觉合理性，缺乏对科学推理和知识驱动合成能力的评估。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建一个需要科学知识和因果推理的视频生成基准，以推动该领域超越视觉逼真度层面的发展。

Method: 构建包含1,253个专家标注示例的基准，覆盖自然科学、医疗健康、人文社科和工程四个学科60个主题；设计基于评分规则的评估协议，验证非专家人类评估者和MLLM-as-Judge系统与专家判断的一致性；对16个前沿模型（含专有和开源模型）进行系统基准测试。

Result: 在评分规则协议下，非专家评估者和MLLM-as-Judge系统与专家判断具有较高一致性；16个模型的自动感知质量分数趋同，但在Prompt Grounding和科学因果正确性上表现差异显著，专有模型与开源模型之间存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 视觉真实感方面的进步尚未转化为对科学和因果动力学的可靠建模能力，表明视频生成领域仍需在科学推理和知识驱动合成方向上继续突破。

Abstract: We introduce Sci-VBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating knowledge- and reasoning-intensive video generation across scientific domains. It contains 1,253 expert-annotated examples spanning 60 subjects across four core disciplines: Natural Science, Healthcare, Humanities & Social Sciences, and Engineering. Each example requires models to generate temporally rich videos that demand scientific reasoning and knowledge-grounded synthesis, going beyond surface-level visual plausibility. We further establish a rubric-based evaluation protocol. Our analysis shows that, under this protocol, both non-expert human evaluators and MLLM-as-Judge systems can achieve relatively high agreement with expert judgments, supporting reproducible evaluation at scale. We benchmark 16 frontier proprietary and open-source models and find that, while automatic perceptual-quality scores cluster tightly across systems, performance on Prompt Grounding and Scientific and Causal Correctness varies substantially, with a pronounced proprietary-open-source gap. These findings show that advances in visual realism have not yet translated into reliable modeling of scientific and causal dynamics.

</details>


### [259] [Financial Numerical Prediction and Allocation as Token Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09880)
*Xu Ouyang,Moontae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FinATOM框架，探索因果语言模型通过约束式token生成直接进行金融数值预测与决策，替代传统的任务特定回归、排序或策略头。在股票收益预测与ETF资产配置两个任务上验证了该方法的可行性，并在2023-2025年回测中取得优于基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融预测依赖任务特定的输出头（回归、排序或策略），将语言模型与实际被评估的数值目标相分离。作者质疑这种分离是否必要，探索能否让因果语言模型通过约束token生成直接表示预测结果与决策，从而实现更统一的建模范式。

Method: FinATOM包含两个统一的无头（head-free）接口：(1) 三步股票收益预测模型——自回归生成波动率标准化收益token，采用序数+排序监督训练，随后进行一个epoch的token级策略微调；(2) 动态五ETF配置模型——生成归一化的多头权重，SFT模仿因果均值-方差基准，并用DAPO增强的GRPO在一致性约束下优化21日实测Sharpe。

Result: 在2023-2025年ETF测试中，配置策略将池化总Sharpe从1.428提升至1.529，考虑5bp交易成本后的净Sharpe从1.394提升至1.494。多模态配置输入达到最高三期平均Sharpe 1.540，2025年优势最显著。在FinTexTS上，SFT和策略方法的累计收益/Sharpe分别达到73.52%/2.68和73.72%/2.69。

Conclusion: 研究结果验证了语言模型通过直接token生成进行金融数值预测与决策的可行性，为统一金融语言模型接口提供了新方向，同时提示需要在更广泛的资产类别、市场状态和随机种子下进行进一步测试。

Abstract: Financial prediction typically relies on task-specific regression, ranking, or policy heads, separating the language model from the numerical object ultimately evaluated. We investigate whether a causal language model can instead represent forecasts and decisions directly through constrained token generation. FinATOM introduces a unified, head-free interface for three-step stock-return forecasting and dynamic five-ETF allocation. The forecasting model autoregressively emits volatility-standardized return tokens and is trained with ordinal and ranking supervision followed by a one-epoch token-level policy stage. The allocation model generates normalized long-only weights; supervised fine-tuning imitates a causal mean--variance anchor, and DAPO-augmented GRPO optimizes realized 21-day Sharpe subject to anchor consistency. In 2023--2025 ETF tests, the allocation policy improves pooled gross Sharpe from 1.428 to 1.529 and net Sharpe under a 5-bp transaction-cost model from 1.394 to 1.494. The multimodal allocation input attains the highest three-period mean Sharpe of 1.540, with its clearest advantage in 2025. On FinTexTS, the SFT and policy strategies achieve 73.52\%/2.68 and 73.72\%/2.69 cumulative-return/Sharpe, respectively. These results support the feasibility of direct language-model token generation for financial numerical prediction and decision-making, while motivating broader tests across assets, regimes, and random seeds.

</details>


### [260] [Space-Creating versus Dead Possession: An Off-Ball Possession-Quality Index for Broadcast Football](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09887)
*Seongjin Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出双层框架来识别足球中的"无效控球"（junk possession）：第一层基于事件的垃圾控球指数，通过峰值威胁增益给控球序列定价，并在平局或落后的比赛状态下标记低威胁序列；第二层通过广播视频投影到球场坐标，计算空间创造指数（SCI）区分"空间已创造但未转化"与"真正无效的控球"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于事件的控球价值框架（如expected threat、VAEP、on-ball value）只对持球动作定价，忽略了无球侧的"空间问题"——持球到底是创造了空间还是死循环？控球率作为最常被引用也最具误导性的足球数据，需要更精细的判断工具。

Method: 两层框架：1) 事件侧"垃圾控球指数"：在expected threat网格下按峰值威胁增益为每个控球序列定价，并通过重建实时比分排除领先保护型控球，在平局或落后状态下标记低威胁序列；2) 对标记窗口，将广播视频投影到球场坐标，计算空间创造指数（SCI），衡量净控球区域变化以判断是否抢占空间或推动对手防线后撤。

Result: 基于2026年FIFA世界杯103场比赛（206个队次），垃圾控球标记与积分的相关系数为r=-0.37，与xG差值为r=-0.51；在固定团队进攻VAEP和场区占优后，垃圾标记仍与积分显著负相关（p<0.0001），而VAEP不再显著。在9场世界杯比赛的35个标记窗口中，74%为非空间创造、19%为弱推进、6%为空间创造窗口。

Conclusion: 该双层框架成功区分了"空间已创造但未转化"与"真正的无效控球"——这是仅依靠事件层面on-ball价值无法做到的区分。垃圾控球指数提供了超越现有on-ball价值模型的增量信息。

Abstract: Ball possession is the most-cited and most-misleading number in football: 60% recycled in one's own half is not 60% spent pinning the opponent back. Existing event-based possession-value frameworks (expected threat, VAEP, on-ball value) price on-ball actions but ignore the off-ball question a sterile possession poses: did holding the ball create space, or was the circulation dead? We answer this in two layers. First, an event-side junk-possession index prices each possession sequence by its peak threat gain under an expected-threat grid and -- after reconstructing the live scoreline to exclude lead-protecting circulation -- flags low-threat sequences in tied-or-losing states. On the 2026 FIFA World Cup (103 matches, 206 team-matches) the flag correlates negatively with points (r=-0.37) and xG difference (r=-0.51, partly index-coupled). It is not a repackaging of on-ball value: with team offensive VAEP and field tilt held fixed, the junk flag stays strongly negatively associated with points (p<0.0001, also match-clustered) while VAEP is not significant -- in this same-match (descriptive) regression it adds information beyond this on-ball action-value model. Second, for a flagged window we resolve whether it was spatially dead or space-creating by projecting broadcast video to pitch coordinates and measuring a Space-Creation Index (SCI): a net pitch-control change capturing whether the possession seized space or pushed the opponent's block back. Across 31 of 35 flagged windows from nine World Cup matches (a purposive sample), 74% are spatially non-space-creating, 19% weak progression, and 6% space-creating windows the event flag alone would score as failure -- including a side with 73% of the ball that exited on penalties (two non-creating windows). The two layers separate space-creating-but-unconverted from sterile possession, a distinction event-only on-ball value cannot make.

</details>


### [261] [DistMoE: Private-data Rehearsal-free Routing in Mixture-of-Experts for Distributed Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09907)
*Mainak Singha,Niccolò Biondi,Elisa Ricci,Subhankar Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DistMoE，一种基于混合专家（MoE）的分布式视觉指令微调方法，通过在语言解码器每层中为各客户端添加私有FFN专家，并采用公共锚定的专家组合阶段和各项同性正则化损失，实现无需跨客户端重演训练即可完成模块化的领域知识组合与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽然具备强大的多模态指令跟随能力，但传统的适应方法通常假设数据集中访问和昂贵的联合训练，无法处理数据分布在私有、领域特定或权限受限的多个客户端场景。

Method: DistMoE在语言解码器的每一层中，用公共的FFN专家加上每个客户端专属的私有FFN专家来获取领域特定知识。为解决独立训练导致的专家表征漂移问题，作者引入公共锚定的专家组合阶段，仅更新路由器和轻量私有投影适配器，在本地客户端数据与公共数据的混合上，通过各项同性正则化损失进行训练，实现免跨客户端训练的协作组合。推理时通过令牌级模块化路由在公共与私有专家间选择，无需显式领域标签。

Result: 在多个视觉-语言基准上的实验表明，DistMoE支持灵活的专家复用、有效的领域自适应，并在保持对客户端特定知识的模块化控制的同时取得具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: DistMoE为分布式、隐私受限的多模态数据场景提供了一种高效的MoE框架，实现了无需集中数据或重演训练的模块化领域适应，兼顾性能与隐私控制。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown strong multimodal instruction-following ability, but adapting them to diverse visual-language domains typically assumes centralized data access and costly joint training. This is restrictive when data is distributed across private, domain-specific, or permission-limited clients. To this end, we propose DistMoE, a mixture-of-experts (MoE) approach for distributed visual instruction tuning. In each layer of the language decoder it augments the public feedforward network (FFN) with a client-specific private FFN expert, with the goal to acquire domain-specific knowledge. However, independent expert training causes the private FFNs to learn representation of different scale and magnitudes, making merging the experts difficult. To reduce client-specific drift, we introduce a public-anchored expert composition stage that updates only routers and lightweight private projection adapters on a mix of local client data and public data, via an isotropic regularization loss, therefore making it cross-client rehearsal-free composition. During inference, DistMoE performs modular routing over public and private experts, enabling token-wise domain composition without explicit domain labels. Experiments across diverse visual-language benchmarks show that DistMoE enables flexible expert reuse, effective domain adaptation, and competitive performance while preserving modular control over client-specific knowledge. Codes are available at https://github.com/mainaksingha01/DistMoE.

</details>


### [262] [Beyond Hazard Resemblance: Contrastive Event Adjudication for Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09908)
*Wenti Yin,Xiang Wang,Huaxin Zhang,Hanqing Wang,Hongbo Shao,Changxin Gao,Nong Sang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CEAVAD，一种无需训练的视频异常检测方法。该方法将推理单元从孤立的异常概念转变为可证伪的事件假设，通过构建"危害-正常"事件对比对，在推理阶段建立可解释的决策边界，实现时间上定位的异常检测和基于证据的解释，在三个基准数据集上达到训练免费范式下的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督方法需要大量目标域标注数据；已有的训练免费方法虽利用预训练模型的语义知识，但异常描述更丰富并不直接等价于更好的异常判定——因为更丰富的描述可能更好地刻画危害相似性却无法解决"是否异常"的判定问题，缺乏明确的异常决策准则。

Method: CEAVAD包含三个步骤：(1) 利用公共安全知识构建危害-正常事件对比对，将每个危害机制与通用正常描述和机制特定的良性对照配对；(2) 判断目标时间区间更支持危害解释还是良性竞争者，生成可修正的对比边界提议；(3) 在竞争性解释之间进行裁决，判断危害假设是否在视频证据中存续，同时支持时间定位的异常检测和基于证据的解释。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的视频异常检测基准数据集上的实验表明，CEAVAD在训练免费范式下取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将异常检测重构为竞争性事件解释之间的裁决过程，CEAVAD在无需任何训练数据的条件下建立了可解释的推理时异常决策边界，验证了以事件假设为推理单元的有效性。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) aims to identify and temporally localize abnormal events in videos. Supervised methods learn anomaly decision boundaries from target-domain annotations but require substantial in-domain data. Existing training-free methods leverage the rich semantic knowledge and reasoning capabilities of pretrained models to interpret visual content, yet these capabilities do not directly define an anomaly decision criterion: richer anomaly descriptions better capture hazard resemblance without resolving abnormality. To this end, we propose Contrastive Event Adjudication for training-free Video Anomaly Detection (CEAVAD), which shifts the unit of inference from isolated anomaly concepts to falsifiable event hypotheses and establishes an inference-time explanatory boundary through the interaction between competing explanations and video evidence. Specifically, CEAVAD first uses public-safety knowledge to construct hazard-benign event contrasts, pairing each hazard mechanism with a generic normal account and a mechanism-specific benign counterpart. It then determines whether the target interval better supports a hazard explanation or its benign competitor, yielding a revisable contrastive boundary proposal for the target. Finally, CEAVAD adjudicates between the competing explanations to determine whether the hazard hypothesis survives the video evidence, supporting both temporally localized anomaly detection and evidence-grounded explanations. Experiments on three widely used VAD benchmarks demonstrate that CEAVAD achieves state-of-the-art performance under the training-free paradigm.

</details>


### [263] [Learning How the World Evolves: Extrapolative Video World Models via Latent Dynamics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09926)
*Haodong Li,Shaoteng Liu,Tianyu Wang,Chongjian Ge,Sihui Ji,Jiahan Zhang,Xin Lin,Haolin Lu,Zhe Lin,Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出潜空间动力学推理（LDR）方法，将视频扩散模型的隐状态转移显式建模为运动学积分过程，仅让模型回归三阶及以上的动力学残差，从而显著提升模型对学到的物理规律的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型主要拟合像素外观，而未显式建模像素随时间的转移规律（即运动学/动力学），因此能生成视觉上合理但物理上不准确的帧，尤其是在训练分布之外的场景下无法真正掌握底层物理规律。

Method: LDR将潜状态的时间转移建模为显式的运动学积分：低阶动力学被数值积分处理，模型只需回归驱动推演的三阶及以上残差项；同时LDR在结构化潜空间而非稠密卷积特征上执行该积分，以提升外推能力。

Result: 在PhyWorld白盒物理基准（五个任务：匀速运动、抛物线、碰撞、反弹、逼近）上，LDR的分布内外误差差距比视频扩散基线小20倍以上；在单任务和联合任务训练下、256²分辨率场景中均表现优异，同时参数量减少26倍、推理速度提升143倍。LDR甚至能在严重偏移下泛化（如仅训练红球左→右运动，可预测蓝方块右→左运动）。

Conclusion: LDR是首个能够将学到的动力学外推到训练分布之外的视频世界模型，证明了在结构化潜空间上进行显式运动学积分能有效提升物理泛化能力。

Abstract: The world evolves following its dynamics, i.e., its laws of motion. However, leading video diffusion models largely fit the pixels without modeling how the pixels transit over time. Thus, they render visually plausible frames but may not accurately obey the laws. To capture the dynamics purely from pixels, we introduce Latent Dynamics Reasoning (LDR). LDR casts the latent transition as an explicit kinematic integration, where the lower-order dynamics are integrated numerically and the model regresses only the third- and higher-order residual that drives the rollout. For this integration to extrapolate better, LDR runs it on a structured latent rather than dense convolutional features. Following PhyWorld, we validate LDR on a controlled white-box physics benchmark spanning five tasks (uniform motion, parabola, collision, bouncing, looming), focusing on out-of-distribution scenarios that reveal whether a model has truly learned the underlying dynamics. LDR extrapolates the learned dynamics far better: the gap between its in- and out-of-distribution error is over 20$\times$ smaller than the video diffusion baseline's, under both single- and joint-task training at 256$^2$ resolution, while using 26$\times$ fewer parameters and running 143$\times$ faster. LDR can even generalize under severe shift: for example, trained only on red balls moving left-to-right, it correctly predicts the motion of a blue square moving right-to-left. To our knowledge, this is the first video world model that extrapolates learned dynamics beyond its training distribution. Project page: https://lat-dyn-reason.github.io/

</details>


### [264] [Perception Before Supervision: Self-Contained Visual Distillation from Counterfactual Blind Spots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09931)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Omkar Thawakar,Ritesh Thawkar,Abdelrahman Shaker,Rao Muhammad Anwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CVPD（对比反事实视觉过程蒸馏），这是首个完全自包含的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）密集、在线策略、token级视觉自蒸馏框架。CVPD通过识别"视觉盲区"（即放大某区域会改变和锐化模型答案分布，而移除该区域对全图行为影响不大的区域），利用三门反事实准则从模型自身响应中识别这些区域，并将其转化为密集对比监督信号进行自蒸馏。在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上，CVPD在12个基准测试中超越6个自进化基线方法（包括依赖外部GPT-4o监督的方法），且无任何回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型的自改进通常依赖基于奖励的方法，但只能提供粗略的标量反馈。蒸馏虽然能提供密集的token级监督，但在视觉领域通常需要依赖外部标注、工具或更强模型构建的特权上下文。作者希望提出一个完全自包含的框架，无需任何外部资源即可实现密集的视觉自蒸馏。

Method: CVPD的核心创新包括：（1）定义"视觉盲区"——放大某区域会改变和锐化模型答案分布，而移除该区域对全图行为影响甚微的区域，这些区域揭示模型能编码但未能在全图条件下稳定利用的感知信息。（2）提出三门反事实准则（Counterfactual Criterion），直接从模型自身响应中识别这些区域。（3）将识别出的区域转化为密集对比监督信号，用于自蒸馏训练。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上，CVPD在12个基准测试中超越6个自进化基线（包括依赖外部GPT-4o监督的方法），且无任何回归。具体提升包括：OCRBench +3.60、MMStar细粒度感知 +3.38、MMStar逻辑推理 +3.08，同时在更广泛的多模态基准上保持或提升性能。

Conclusion: CVPD证明了无需任何外部标注、工具或更强模型，仅利用模型自身的响应即可实现有效的密集token级视觉自蒸馏。通过识别"视觉盲区"并将其转化为对比监督信号，模型能够更好地利用已被编码但未充分利用的感知信息，从而在多模态基准上获得一致且显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Self-improvement for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is typically driven by reward-based methods that provide only coarse scalar feedback. Distillation offers a richer alternative through dense token-level supervision, but in the visual domain it usually depends on privileged context constructed using external annotations and tools, or stronger models. We introduce \textbf{CVPD} (Contrastive Counterfactual Visual Process Distillation), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully self-contained framework for dense, on-policy, token-level visual self-distillation for MLLMs. CVPD identifies visual blind spots where zooming into a region changes and sharpens the model's answer distribution, while removing the same region leaves the full-image behavior largely unchanged. Such regions reveal perceptual information that the model can encode but fails to consistently utilize under full-image conditioning. We propose a three-gate Counterfactual Criterion that identifies these regions directly from the model's own responses and converts them into dense contrastive supervision for self-distillation. On Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, CVPD outperforms six self-evolving baselines across twelve benchmarks, including methods that rely on external GPT-4o supervision, without a single regression. It achieves gains of $+3.60$ on OCRBench, $+3.38$ on MMStar Fine-Grained Perception, and $+3.08$ on MMStar Logical Reasoning, while maintaining or improving performance on broader multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [Unified Hallucination Fuzzing for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07525)
*Pengfei Zhou,Jiajun Song,Zhiwei Tang,Yixing Ma,Xiaopeng Peng,Donghui Si,Yuhang Xu,Huiqi Song,Yiyuan Miao,Yichen Qian,Weihua Chen,Wangbo Zhao,Bohan Zhuang,Jiasheng Tang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个系统性评估框架，整合了基于统一分类法的细粒度基准UniHall与自适应的多模态模糊测试框架SAMF，用于动态评估和压力测试MLLM的幻觉问题，发现SOTA模型在模糊测试下性能显著下降，并揭示了有用性与幻觉之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM幻觉评估主要依赖静态基准，存在分类覆盖狭窄和性能快速饱和的问题，无法反映模型在真实世界动态场景中的鲁棒性，亟需更全面和动态的评估方法。

Method: 构建UniHall细粒度数据集（覆盖Object、Instruction、Knowledge三个维度），提出Self-Adaptive Multimodal Fuzzing（SAMF）框架，采用进化变异策略探索模型幻觉边界，并通过多模态oracle集成的结构化指标套件确保动态输入评估的可靠性。

Result: 实验表明SOTA MLLM在模糊测试下相比传统设置出现显著性能退化，暴露了推理能力与事实基础之间的脱节；同时发现强化学习对齐会加剧指令遵循任务中的谄媚行为，存在有用性-幻觉权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架弥补了静态基准的不足，为MLLM幻觉提供了动态、系统化的评估手段，揭示了当前对齐策略可能牺牲事实准确性来换取有用性的重要问题。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a persistent challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), severely limiting their reliability in high-stakes applications. Existing evaluations, predominantly based on static benchmarks, suffer from narrow taxonomical coverage and rapid performance saturation, failing to reflect model robustness in evolving real-world scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present a systematic evaluation framework integrating a comprehensive benchmark with self-evolving stress testing. First, we introduce UniHall, a fine-grained dataset grounded in a unified taxonomy spanning Object, Instruction, and Knowledge dimensions. Second, to address benchmark saturation, we propose Self-Adaptive Multimodal Fuzzing (SAMF), a self-adaptive framework that employs evolutionary mutation strategies to explore the boundaries of model hallucinations. Crucially, to ensure reliable assessment of dynamic inputs, SAMF incorporates a structured metric suite driven by an ensemble of multi-modal oracles. Our extensive experiments reveal that state-of-the-art MLLMs exhibit significant performance degradation under fuzzing compared to conventional settings, exposing a dissociation between reasoning capabilities and factual grounding. Furthermore, we identify a helpfulness-hallucination trade-off, where reinforcement learning alignment inadvertently exacerbates sycophancy in instruction-following tasks. The framework, code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/LanceZPF/EvalHall.

</details>


### [266] [DocAtlas: Long-Document Understanding as Mutable-State Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07527)
*Hongchen Wei,Yuanzhe Wang,Bei Liu,Yifan Yang,Qi Dai,Kai Qiu,Yunsheng Li,Dongdong Chen,Chong Luo,Zhenzhong Chen,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DocAtlas是一个将长文档理解视为可变状态信息寻求过程的系统，通过外部文档工具环境（搜索、阅读、笔记、回顾）来管理证据收集，在固定上下文预算下结合自改进检索、选择性证据访问和主动工作记忆。使用GPT-5.4在MMLongBench-Doc上达到71.4%（超过人类专家65.8%），用RL训练的Qwen3.5-4B小模型也达到63.7%，较基线提升近10个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强系统通常从静态索引中选择证据，而代理系统虽有多轮工具使用但依赖冻结的专有模型，行为由提示词设定，缺乏可迭代优化的可变状态处理机制，难以有效处理跨多页面、布局、表格、图表的长文档证据联合推理任务。

Method: DocAtlas构建一个可变文档工具环境（mutable document harness），作为外部环境决定每一步搜索、阅读、存储、回顾和展示哪些文档信息。系统维护分层树和笔记存储，代理记录证据时同步更新。支持推理时的大VLM使用和端到端强化学习训练紧凑VLM代理，在固定上下文预算下实现自改进检索、选择性证据访问和主动工作记忆。

Result: 在MMLongBench-Doc基准上，DocAtlas配合GPT-5.4达到71.4%的准确率，超过人类专家参考值65.8%；使用Qwen3.5-4B VLM通过端到端RL训练达到63.7%，远高于54.4%的直接输入基线，证明了可变文档工具环境设计能大幅提升紧凑文档代理性能。

Conclusion: DocAtlas表明将长文档理解建模为可变状态信息寻求过程是有效的，其可变文档工具环境设计既能提升大型VLM的推理性能，也能通过端到端RL显著增强紧凑模型的文档理解能力，且不依赖大规模参数或专有模型。

Abstract: Long-document understanding requires models to find and combine evidence across many pages, layouts, tables, figures, and charts. Existing retrieval-augmented systems usually select evidence from a static index before generation, while recent agentic systems add multi-turn tool use but often rely on frozen proprietary backbones whose behavior is set by prompts. We present DocAtlas, a system that treats long-document understanding as a mutable-state information-seeking process. We instantiate DocAtlas as a mutable document harness: an external environment that determines what document information is searched, read, stored, reviewed, and shown to the model at each step. Given a document and question, the harness exposes search, reading, note-taking, and review tools, maintains a hierarchical tree and note store, and updates both as the agent records evidence. DocAtlas combines self-improving retrieval, selective evidence access, and active working memory under a fixed context budget. The same harness supports inference-time use with large VLMs and end-to-end reinforcement learning for compact VLM agents. With GPT-5.4, DocAtlas reaches 71.4\% on MMLongBench-Doc, exceeding the human-expert reference of 65.8\%. A Qwen3.5-4B VLM trained with end-to-end RL in the DocAtlas environment reaches 63.7\%, compared with a 54.4\% direct-input baseline, showing that mutable document-harness design can improve compact document agents by a large margin.

</details>


### [267] [WuYuEval: A Multi-Level Benchmark for Large Language Models in Solid Waste Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07529)
*Yi Zhang,Hongyang Wang,Zheng Hao Leong,Zihao Wu,Kaijun Lin,Zhixing Pan,Qixun Huangfu,Wei Ren,Wenyan Wu,Fangyun Wang,Wenting Yu,Hengyu Lin,Muling Yang,Zongguo Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了WuYuEval，一个面向大语言模型（LLM）在固体废物管理（SWM）领域的多层级基准测试，包含基础模块（4,590道选择题）和专家模块（247道情景开放式问题），并对33个LLM进行了系统评估，发现模型在高难度推理和专家决策任务上表现显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要评估LLM的通用知识，缺乏对工程、环境和政策约束下专业决策能力的评估，导致LLM在固体废物管理领域的实际能力难以准确衡量。

Method: 构建WuYuEval多层级基准：基础模块包含4,590道覆盖6种任务类型和8个领域类别的选择题；专家模块包含247道涉及多目标优化、约束权衡和系统设计的情景开放式问题。专家任务采用锚定校准的LLM-as-a-Judge评分结合Elo成对比较方法，并对33个LLM进行评估。

Result: 领先模型在基础模块达到94.64%准确率，但平均准确率从简单题的84.14%降至难题的42.50%；模型在计算、实验设计、城市规划和开放式专家任务上表现较弱。推理导向的Thinking模式能改善多数匹配模型对的表现，但增益取决于基础能力且并非一致正向。

Conclusion: 可感知的推理过程只有在紧扣单位、假设和工程约束时才有帮助，否则可能偏离关键答案边界。WuYuEval为开发面向固体废物管理的专业基础模型提供了评估资源和实证基础，强调了专业推理链和显式约束控制的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as technical assistants, but their competence in solid waste management (SWM) remains difficult to assess because existing benchmarks emphasize general knowledge rather than professional decisions under engineering, environmental, and policy constraints. We introduce WuYuEval, a multi-level benchmark for evaluating LLMs in SWM across foundational knowledge, domain reasoning, and expert decision-making. After quality auditing, WuYuEval contains a Foundation Module with 4,590 closed-ended multiple-choice questions across six task types and eight domain categories, together with an Expert Module with 247 scenario-based open-ended questions involving multi-objective optimization, constraint trade-offs, and system design. For expert tasks, we combine anchor-calibrated LLM-as-a-Judge scoring with Elo-based pairwise comparison. Across 33 LLMs, performance varied widely. The leading model reached 94.64\% accuracy on the Foundation Module, but average accuracy still fell from 84.14\% on easy questions to 42.50\% on hard questions, with lower performance concentrated in calculation, experimental design, urban planning, and open-ended expert tasks. Reasoning-oriented Thinking modes improve most matched model pairs after auditing, but the gains depend on baseline capability and are not uniformly positive. These results suggest that visible deliberation helps only when it remains anchored to units, assumptions, and engineering constraints; otherwise, it may drift from decisive answer boundaries. WuYuEval therefore provides both an evaluation resource and an empirical basis for developing SWM-oriented foundation models with professional reasoning chains and explicit constraint control.

</details>


### [268] [Search-G1: Grounded Search Agents via Representation-Based Intrinsic Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07531)
*Cheng Ruoxi,Ma Haoxuan,Zhang Hongyi,Zhang Junming,Duan Ranjie,Xia Qiaolin,Wang Hao,Lu Yu,Shi Haibo,Ma Xingjun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Search-G1，一种基于表示的内在奖励框架，通过两个干预校准的读出来衡量智能体回答的操作性依据（grounding），在不需要过程注释或LLM评判推理的情况下，改善搜索增强语言智能体的检索-依据权衡，生成更短的响应轨迹并保持有竞争力的任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索增强语言智能体应仅在必要时检索外部信息并基于检索证据作答。现有外部奖励要么是稀疏的结果监督，要么需要昂贵的过程注释或LLM评判；而基于策略侧信号（如熵、似然、信息增益）的内部奖励虽然廉价，但主要反映模型置信度而非证据依据。需要一个既廉价又能衡量证据依据的奖励机制。

Method: Search-G1框架包含两个干预校准的读出来衡量操作性依据：（1）提示状态读出（prompt-state readout）预测闭卷充分性，其补集定义策略相关的检索必要性；（2）答案承诺读出（answer-commit readout）通过答案阶段对证据删除的敏感性来估计证据依赖。在策略优化过程中，奖励评分无需过程注释或LLM评判推理；由于RL改变策略表示，Search-G1定期在最新检查点的轨迹上重新拟合两个读出，使奖励与策略共同演化。

Result: 在多个基于搜索的问答基准和两种模型规模上的实验表明，Search-G1改善了依据-搜索成本权衡，在保持竞争性任务准确率的同时产生更短的响应侧轨迹。

Conclusion: Search-G1通过表示级的内在奖励，在没有昂贵外部信号的情况下实现了更好的检索依据与搜索成本之间的权衡，并且代码已开源。

Abstract: Search-augmented language agents should retrieve external information only when necessary and ground their answers in retrieved evidence. Existing external rewards provide either sparse outcome supervision or richer feedback from process annotations and LLM judges. Outcome rewards scale readily but cannot distinguish grounded retrieval from redundant search, whereas richer signals require costly annotation or inference during training. Internal rewards based on policy-side signals such as entropy, likelihood, or information gain are graded and inexpensive to evaluate, yet mainly reflect model confidence rather than evidence grounding. We propose Search-G1, a representation-based intrinsic reward framework that measures the operational grounding of an agent's answers through two intervention-calibrated readouts. A prompt-state readout predicts closed-book sufficiency, whose complement defines policy-relative retrieval necessity; an answer-commit readout estimates evidence reliance from answer-stage sensitivity to evidence deletion. Together, they provide additional credit to correct searched trajectories when retrieval is estimated necessary and the answer is evidence-sensitive, favor correct direct answers when closed-book knowledge suffices, and penalize repeated search. After calibration, reward scoring requires neither process annotations nor LLM-as-judge inference during policy optimization. Because reinforcement learning changes policy representations, Search-G1 periodically refits both readouts on trajectories from the latest checkpoint, allowing the reward to co-evolve with the policy. Experiments across multiple search-based question-answering benchmarks and two model scales show that Search-G1 improves the grounding--search-cost trade-off, producing shorter response-side trajectories at competitive task accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/Rosy0912/Search-G1.

</details>


### [269] [Scaling Inherently Interpretable Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07594)
*Guide Labs Team,Andreas Madsen,Aya Abdelsalam Ismail,Giang Nguyen,Isaac Plant,Muawiz Chaudhary,Nathaniel Monson,Saqib Azim,Zhichen Guo,Julius Adebayo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"可解释性是能力的负担"这一传统观点，提出将可解释性作为训练管线的约束条件，与语言建模目标联合优化。实验表明在三个数量级的计算规模下，可解释性与能力同步提升，而非相互制约。作者通过Steerling-8B——一个带因果注意力掩码的扩散语言模型——实例化了该训练时配方，实现了对生成令牌归因于输入令牌、人类可理解概念和训练数据的能力，并支持闭环干预（诊断-检索-概念引导纠正）。该模型在少2-16倍算力下仍与开放同类模型竞争，表明可解释性可以内建于训练中并随规模提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释性方法通常是事后进行（post-hoc），其可靠性难以验证，且被认为会牺牲模型能力。作者希望扭转这一范式——将可解释性从"事后负担"转变为"训练约束"，并探究可解释性与模型能力在规模化过程中是否真的此消彼长。

Method: 将可解释性作为训练管线的正则化/约束条件，与语言建模目标联合优化；在自回归和扩散两类语言模型上，跨越三个数量级的计算规模进行系统验证。具体实例化为Steerling-8B，一个带因果注意力掩码的扩散语言模型，支持令牌级别归因（输入令牌、概念、训练数据），并实现概念引导干预的闭环机制。

Result: 1) 可解释性随模型规模提升而改善——表征变得更解耦、更对齐人类可理解概念；2) Steerling-8B在远低于同类模型的算力下（2-16倍差距）仍保持竞争力；3) 闭环干预机制（归因诊断→检索训练数据→概念引导纠正）无需重训练即可修正模型行为。

Conclusion: 可解释性不必是能力的代价，它可以被设计进训练过程中，且与模型能力同步扩展。这提出了一种新的扩展范式：可解释性内建于训练，并随规模增长而增强，而非事后修补。

Abstract: Interpretability is often treated as a tax on capability: language models are trained as opaque systems, then explained after the fact, with methods whose reliability is difficult to establish. In this work, we challenge this premise. Rather than reverse-engineering a model, we make interpretability a constraint of the training pipeline, optimized alongside the language modeling objective. Across three orders of magnitude of compute, on both autoregressive and diffusion language models, interpretability scales with capability rather than against it. Surprisingly, model representations become more disentangled and aligned with human-understandable concepts with scale.
  We instantiate the training-time recipe with Steerling-8B, a diffusion language model with a causal attention mask. For any group of generated tokens, Steerling-8B attributes the output to relevant input tokens, human-understandable concepts, and training data. This enables closed-loop intervention: diagnose an output through its concept or feature attribution, retrieve similar training data, and correct the behavior through concept steering without retraining. Steerling-8B remains competitive with open peer models trained on substantially 2-16x more compute, suggesting a different scaling paradigm: interpretability can be designed into training, and it improves with scale.

</details>


### [270] [Embedding Initialization for Unseen Low-resource Languages in Multilingual NMT: A Case Study on Limbum-English Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07629)
*Samiratu Ntohsi,Neza David Tuyishimire,Anesu Kafesu,Marvin Ogore,Samuel Oluwajunwonlo Babalola,Oche Ankeli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多语言嵌入均值初始化策略，用于为NLLB-200等多语言机器翻译模型选择无监督语言代理token，在Limbum-英语翻译任务上达到与最佳单语言代理相当的性能（chrF2++≈46.7 vs 47.3），远超零样本和从头训练的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLLB-200覆盖200种语言但仍遗漏数千种语言（包括喀麦隆的大部分Grassfields Bantu语言）。在微调这些模型处理未见语言时，需要选择代理语言token，但目前缺乏原理性的选择方法。

Method: 提出一种嵌入初始化策略：将模型中多个类型学相关语言的token嵌入取平均值，作为新语言token的初始嵌入。在Limbum-英语翻译上使用8,837句对的平行语料（来自新约文本和双语词典）进行评测，并与零样本、从头训练Transformer、Swahili单代理token微调等方法对比。

Result: 多语言嵌入均值初始化达到chrF2++=46.7，与最佳单语言代理（Swahili，47.3）相当，比零样本（12.5）和从头训练（14.5）高32+分。但所有系统均无法保留声调变音符号。

Conclusion: 多语言迁移是极低资源Bantu翻译中的主导因素，所提出的方法消除了启发式代理选择的需求，但仍存在声调变音符号保留这一开放挑战。数据集和代码已公开。

Abstract: Multilingual neural machine translation models such as NLLB-200 cover 200 languages but leave thousands unsupported, including most Grassfields Bantu languages of Cameroon. When fine-tuning these models for an unseen language, practitioners must choose a proxy language token, yet no principled method exists for this selection. We implemented an embedding initialization strategy where a language token is the average of embeddings from multiple typologically related languages already in the mod el. We evaluate this approach on Limbum-to-English translation using a parallel corpus of 8,837 sentence pairs from New Testament text and a bilingual dictionary. We compare models: NLLB-200 zero-shot (chrF2++ = 12.5), a Transformer trained from scratch (chrF2++ = 14.5), NLLB-200 fine-tuned with a Swahili proxy token (chrF2++ = 47.3), and NLLB-200 with our averaged embedding initialization (chrF2++ = 46.7). We find that the multi-language initialization achieves performance comparable to the best single-language proxy. Both NLLB-200 variants improve over the from-scratch baseline by over 32 chrF2++ points. These results show that multilingual transfer is the dominant factor in extremely low-resource Bantu translation while eliminating the need for heuristic proxy selection. However, all systems fail to preserve tonal diacritics, highlighting an open challenge. We make our dataset and code available to support further research.

</details>


### [271] [SurveyReview: A Reviewer-Aligned Benchmark for Survey Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07641)
*Yuheng Zhang,Yuanchun Wang,Fanjin Zhang,Ruyu Zhao,Juanzi Li,Jie Tang,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SurveyReview，一个与审稿人对齐的多维度综述论文评估基准和数据集，包含675篇综述论文和1630份审稿报告，并开发了SurveyAlign基线评估器（基于Qwen3-32B微调），将评估与人类审稿人的对齐误差（MSE）从2.28降至1.38。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型自动化生成综述论文的规模扩大，评估成为瓶颈。现有方法依赖现成的LLM-as-a-judge方案，缺乏与人类审稿人的系统对齐，且缺少量化评估对齐程度的系统框架。

Method: 收集并标注675篇综述论文的1630份审稿报告，将自由文本评论转化为四个维度（可读性、批判性、全面性、结构）的评分及支持理由；发布标准化训练/测试划分和评估协议；基于Qwen3-32B使用LoRA微调开发SurveyAlign基线评估器，并结合外部知识增强知识密集型维度的评估。

Result: 在测试集上，SurveyAlign相比GPT-5.2的提示式评判显著提升了与审稿人的对齐度，四个维度的平均MSE从2.28降至1.38，MAE从1.15降至0.69。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个多维度、与审稿人对齐的综述评估数据集及可复现的评估框架，并提供了强基线评估器，为未来综述评估研究提供了竞争性参考和基准。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large language models has transformed survey writing from a months-long manual effort into an automated process. As generation scales, reliable evaluation becomes the bottleneck, and LLMs are increasingly used as survey evaluators. However, existing approaches largely rely on off-the-shelf LLM-as-a-judge methods without systematic alignment to human reviewers, and there remains a lack of systematic frameworks for quantifying alignment with human reviewers. To address this gap, we propose SurveyReview, a reviewer-aligned, multi-dimensional benchmark and dataset for survey evaluation. We collect and annotate 675 survey papers with 1,630 review reports. We structure authentic peer-review reports by converting free-form comments into four-dimensional scores (Readability, Criticalness, Comprehensiveness, Structure) paired with supporting rationales. We further release standardized train/test splits and an evaluation protocol to measure alignment between automatic evaluators and human reviewers. To validate the benchmark, we develop SurveyAlign, a strong baseline evaluator by fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with LoRA on our annotated data, augmented with external knowledge for knowledge-intensive dimensions. On the test set, SurveyAlign substantially improves reviewer alignment over prompt-based judging with GPT-5.2, reducing average MSE from 2.28 to 1.38 and MAE from 1.15 to 0.69 across all four dimensions. Our contributions are twofold: (1) we establish the first multi-dimensional, reviewer-aligned dataset with a reproducible evaluation framework for survey reviewing; (2) we develop a strong baseline evaluator that substantially improves alignment with human reviewers, providing a competitive reference for future research. Our code and data are available at https://surveyreview.github.io

</details>


### [272] [Evaluating Dedicated Monolingual and Joint Multilingual Causal Models for Dravidian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07727)
*Venkata Naga Sai Vishnu Rohit Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了达罗毗荼语系（泰米尔语、泰卢固语、卡纳达语、马拉雅拉姆语）在预训练语言模型中的表现。作者从零训练了五个GPT-2架构模型：四个单语模型（每种语言各一个，使用32K词表）和一个多语言共享模型（64K词表），与mGPT进行对比。结果显示单语模型在情感分类和命名实体识别上优于mGPT，且其分词器效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 达罗毗荼语系语言在多语言模型的训练数据中占比很小，因此不清楚这些模型对各语言的实际能力如何。作者希望评估单语与多语模型在资源较少语言上的表现差异。

Method: 从零训练五个GPT-2架构模型：四个单语模型（各语言独立，32K词表子词分词器）和一个多语言共享模型（64K词表）。所有模型使用清洗后的CC-100、Wikipedia和Samanantar数据训练。通过困惑度、每字节比特数、分词器效率和微调结果进行评估，并与mGPT对比。

Result: 单语模型在情感分类和命名实体识别任务上优于mGPT；单语模型的分词器在所有测试语言上均比多语言共享模型的分词器更高效。

Conclusion: 在低资源语言（达罗毗荼语系）场景下，单语模型相比多语言模型具有更好的性能和分词效率，表明针对特定语言训练模型可能更适合资源有限的语种。

Abstract: Dravidian languages, mainly Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam make up only a small part of the data used to train multilingual language models, so it's not clear how much per-language ability these models actually keep. I have trained five GPT-2 architecture models from scratch to compare four monolingual models (one each for Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam, each with its own 32K-vocabulary subword tokenizer) against one multilingual model sharing a 64K-vocabulary subword tokenizer across all four languages. All the 5 models are trained on cleaned CC-100, Wikipedia, and Samanantar data. I have tested the models on perplexity, bits-per-byte, tokenizer efficiency, and fine-tuning results which are compared against mGPT. The monolingual models outperform mGPT on sentiment classification and named entity recognition, and their tokenizers proved more efficient than the shared multilingual model across all the languages tested.

</details>


### [273] [The No-Meaning Falsity: The Structural Impossibility of the Arbitrary Sign in Classical Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07737)
*Elnaserledinellah Mahmoud Abdelwahab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过建立古典阿拉伯语非连接形态学的形式数学模型，证明了其词汇意义由不变的词根和形态句法模式之间的交互决定，从而驳斥了后现代主义关于语义无限不确定性的主张。文章通过形态对应定理和语义定位定理论证了阿拉伯语意义在派生层面已确定，并利用条件Kolmogorov复杂度形式化相对任意性，证明阿拉伯语相对任意性可判定且小于1，与印欧语言形成严格的系统复杂性不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验后现代主义声称的无限语义不确定性及其索绪尔任意符号基石的合理性，即这些声称是否与古典阿拉伯语的结构架构兼容。

Method: 采用形式数学建模方法，构建阿拉伯语非连接形态学的数学模型，建立形态对应定理和语义定位定理，并利用条件Kolmogorov复杂度对索绪尔的相对任意性进行算法化形式定义（"无规则性"）。

Result: 证明了普遍相对任意性在形式上是不可判定的，而阿拉伯语的相对任意性是可判定的，且其受动机能指（W和M层级）的相对任意性可证明小于1，从而在系统复杂度上与印欧语言形成严格不对称。

Conclusion: 古典阿拉伯语的结构证明了语义确定性和有限任意性，对后现代主义的语义无限不确定性主张构成形式上的反驳。

Abstract: This paper investigates whether the postmodern claim of unrestricted semantic indeterminacy, and its foundational Saussurean axiom of the arbitrary sign, are compatible with the structural architecture of Classical Arabic. We develop a formal mathematical model of Arabic non concatenative morphology in which lexical meaning is determined by the interaction between an invariant root and a morphosyntactic pattern. Within this framework, we establish a Morphological Correspondence Theorem, demonstrating that every lexical item is uniquely generated by a root pattern pair, and a Semantic Localization Theorem, proving that lexical meaning is determined at the derivational level prior to surface realization. To address Saussurean weaker notion of relative arbitrariness, we formalize it via conditional Kolmogorov complexity, defining arbitrariness algorithmically as the no rule property. We prove that general relative arbitrariness is formally undecidable, while Arabic relative arbitrariness is decidable and provably less than 1 for its motivated signifiers (Levels W and M), establishing a strict system complexity asymmetry over Indo-European languages.

</details>


### [274] [Jako Tako or Fluent? Presenting PoVisLE: A Polish Vision-Language Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07763)
*Anna Kołos,Grzegorz Statkiewicz,Karolina Seweryn,Katarzyna Kowol,Karolina Piosek,Wojciech Kusa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PoVisLE，一个面向波兰语的单文化视觉语言基准测试集，包含1,117张图片和2,366个手工标注的VQA问答对，旨在评估视觉语言模型在特定文化语境下的深层多模态理解能力，超越表面的视觉识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型主要基于英语中心化数据进行训练，在处理具有文化特定含义的视觉内容时表现不佳。已有的文化能力基准往往基于模板驱动且停留于表面识别层面，无法评估模型在文化情境中的深层语言和语用理解能力。

Method: 作者构建了PoVisLE基准数据集，采用"接地评估"范式（grounded evaluation paradigm），即语言需在与视觉上下文的交互中进行解释。数据集包含1,117张图片和2,366个手工标注的VQA问答对，专为波兰文化背景设计。

Result: 该数据集提供了一个受控且具有挑战性的资源，可用于评估视觉语言模型在文化接地条件下的多模态理解能力，有效暴露当前模型在表面识别之外的深层理解缺陷。

Conclusion: PoVisLE为评估视觉语言模型的文化接地理解能力提供了可靠的基准资源，填补了现有文化基准仅关注表面识别的空白，推动文化多模态理解研究向更深层的语用和情境解释方向发展。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on tasks such as image captioning, visual question answering, and image-to-text generation. However, they are predominantly trained on English-centric data, which limits their ability to handle culturally grounded visual understanding and leads to failures in interpreting region-specific meanings, symbolic content, and context-dependent visual cues. Existing benchmarks for cultural competence are often template-driven and focused on surface-level recognition, making them insufficient for evaluating deeper linguistic and pragmatic understanding in culturally situated settings. We introduce PoVisLE, a monocultural vision-language benchmark for Polish designed to evaluate culturally grounded multimodal understanding under a grounded evaluation paradigm, where language is interpreted in interaction with visual context. The dataset contains 1,117 images and 2,366 manually annotated VQA pairs. Overall, our dataset provides a controlled and challenging resource for assessing culturally grounded vision-language understanding beyond surface-level recognition.

</details>


### [275] [On the use of foundation models in cognitive science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07812)
*Raj Sanjay Shah,Alex Warstadt,Michael Frank,Sashank Varma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个四阶段的推理框架，用于评估Foundation Models（FMs）作为认知和发展模型的合理性，强调仅靠行为对齐不足以证明FMs的解释性价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量研究评估FMs在认知和发展层面的对齐程度，但将其作为候选认知模型存在方法论和概念上的重大挑战，关键问题在于什么条件下行为对齐能证明FMs是认知的解释性模型。

Method: 提出了一个四阶段推理框架：（1）将人类实验任务改编为模型兼容格式；（2）指定将模型输出映射到人类测量指标的链接假设；（3）评估行为对应性；（4）在候选模型或操控之间进行比较。

Result: 本文阐明了链接假设在映射模型输出到人类行为测量中的关键作用，识别了限制对齐主张的挑战，并提出了理论驱动和比较性评估的原则。

Conclusion: 行为拟合本身是不够的；只有将对齐嵌入明确的理论承诺、理论诊断性任务以及跨候选模型的系统性对比评估中，对齐才具有科学意义。

Abstract: A host of recent studies have evaluated the cognitive and developmental alignment of Foundation Models (FMs). These investigations include evaluations of their correspondence to adult performance across a range of cognitive domains, as well as whether aspects of model training track children's cognitive development. However, using FMs as candidate cognitive models poses significant methodological and conceptual challenges. A key question underlies this effort: under what conditions does behavioral alignment justify treating FMs as explanatory models of cognition? In this paper, we articulate a four-stage inferential framework for evaluating FMs as cognitive and developmental models: adapting human experimental tasks to model-compatible formats, specifying linking hypotheses that map model outputs to human measures, evaluating behavioral correspondence, and comparing across candidate models or manipulations. We clarify the role of linking hypotheses in mapping model outputs to human behavioral measures, identify challenges that constrain alignment claims, and propose principles for theory-driven and comparative evaluation. Throughout, we argue that behavioral fit alone is insufficient. Alignment becomes scientifically meaningful only when embedded within explicit theoretical commitments, theory-diagnostic tasks, and systematic contrastive evaluation across candidate models.

</details>


### [276] ["Many Are My Names": The Anatomy of the Assistant and Its Personas via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07852)
*Adelaide Danilov,Aria Nourbakhsh,Oleksandr Marchenko Breneur,Salima Lamsiyah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究了语言模型内部如何表征说话者（Assistant、角色扮演人设和叙述故事角色），通过稀疏自编码器在三种生成场景中提取特征，发现Assistant和角色扮演人设共享核心特征并逐层分化，而故事角色则缺乏该核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型如何内部表征"谁在说话"这一问题尚未被充分探索，理解不同说话者角色（助手、角色扮演、故事角色）的内部表征机制对于可解释性和模型控制具有重要意义。

Method: 使用用户表达情感文本及对应模型响应构建数据集，将三种生成场景（助手、角色扮演、故事）分解为稀疏自编码器特征，在轮次边界和代词位置提取，通过过滤管道选择不同深度的特征，并通过引导效应和激活分布来表征每个保留的特征。

Result: 主要发现是助手和角色扮演人设并非独立的替代方案：角色扮演人设保留助手相关特征核心，同时从操作机制层面向行为和风格特征逐层分化；生成的故事角色则缺乏助手相关核心。故事和角色扮演都可以通过"沉浸式模拟模式"与助手区分，但助手在默认设置下有时也会进入或缓慢漂移进入该模式。

Conclusion: 研究表明说话者表征并非简单的独立编码，助手与角色扮演之间具有共享特征核心的层级分化关系，而故事角色是更独立的表征。该发现有助于理解语言模型的人格切换机制和潜在的模式漂移风险。

Abstract: How a language model internally represents who is speaking, the Assistant, an assigned roleplay persona, or a narrated story character, remains underexplored. We study speaker representations using a dataset of user-expressed emotional text and corresponding model responses. We decompose three generation settings (Assistant, Roleplay, and Story) into sparse autoencoder features extracted at turn-boundary and pronoun-token positions and selected through a filtering pipeline for different depths. We characterize each surviving feature through its steering effects and activation distribution. Our main finding is that the Assistant and roleplay personas are not independent alternatives: personas retain the Assistant-associated feature core while progressively differentiating from it across layers, starting from operational machinery towards behavioral and stylistic features. Meanwhile, generated story characters lack the Assistant-associated core. Both Story and Roleplay can be distinguished from the Assistant with Immersive Simulation Mode. However, the Assistant can sometimes enter or slowly drift into it even in the default setting.

</details>


### [277] [SurakshaEval: An Indic Safety Benchmark for Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07862)
*Debopriyo Banerjee,Kapil Rajesh Kavitha,Angana Borah,Xudong Han,Yuxia Wang,Parameswari Krishnamurthy,Utkarsh Agarwal,Atharva Kulkarni,Swaran Lata,Ayush Munot,Dhruv Sahnan,Aaryamonvikram Singh,Preslav Nakov,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SurakshaEval，一个面向10种主要印度语言及英语的多语言安全评估基准，包含人工撰写的真实场景提示，并评测了多种SOTA LLM，发现即使强大的多语言模型在印度语言（尤其是母语脚本）场景下也难以满足细微的安全要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM安全评估数据集主要集中在英语和西方语境，忽略了其他语言中的语言多样性和文化特有的安全风险，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 构建覆盖10种印度语言（阿萨姆语、孟加拉语、古吉拉特语、印地语、卡纳达语、马拉雅拉姆语、马拉地语、旁遮普语、泰米尔语、泰卢固语）及英语的SurakshaEval基准，包含通用提示和区域/语言特有提示，评测多种SOTA LLM并分析失败模式。

Result: 实验表明，即使强大的多语言LLM在印度语言环境中也难以可靠满足细微的安全要求，尤其在母语脚本下表现不佳；识别出过度拒绝、隐式偏见检测缺失、区域敏感场景下上下文意识不足等主要失败模式。

Conclusion: 迫切需要建立纳入区域特定数据和结构化评估协议的安全评估框架，以支持AI系统在不同社会价值观下安全、合乎伦理地开发和部署。

Abstract: Existing safety evaluation datasets for large language models (LLMs) predominantly focus on English and Western contexts, often overlooking the linguistic diversity and culturally grounded safety risks present in other languages. To address this gap, we introduce SurakshaEval, a novel safety benchmark composed of human-written prompts spanning real-world scenarios, explicitly designed for ten major Indian languages - Assamese, Bengali, Gujarati, Hindi, Kannada, Malayalam, Marathi, Punjabi, Tamil, and Telugu, along with English. SurakshaEval includes both generic prompts common across India and region- and language-specific prompts that capture localized sociocultural sensitivities. We benchmark a broad range of state-of-the-art LLMs on SurakshaEval, establish baseline safety performance, and identify recurring failure modes, including over-refusal, missed detection of implicit bias, and insufficient contextual awareness in regionally sensitive settings. Our results show that even strong multilingual LLMs struggle to reliably meet nuanced safety requirements when operating in Indic languages, particularly in native scripts. These findings highlight the urgent need for safety evaluation frameworks that incorporate region-specific data and structured assessment protocols, enabling the development and deployment of AI systems that operate securely, ethically, and in alignment with diverse societal values. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/debobanerjee/SurakshaEval. Warning: This paper contains text that may be offensive or unsafe.

</details>


### [278] [Detection of Self-Introductions in Legislative Testimony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07891)
*Sofija Dimitrijevic,Pallavi Das,Kasey Liu,Foaad Khosmood*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于检测立法委员会听证会中自我介绍的机器学习流水线，利用150万+条话语训练决策树、随机森林和XGBoost分类器，结合BERT特征增强后F1达到0.9782。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正确检测自我介绍并提取发言人姓名，对政府会议场景下的说话人识别任务有重要帮助，因此需要自动化解决方案。

Method: 构建包含154万个话语的训练数据集（涵盖五个州的立法会议），使用姓名匹配启发式方法自动生成标签；设计结合词袋、位置上下文、结构信号、介绍短语指标和话语上下文特征的特征集；训练决策树、随机森林和XGBoost三种分类器，并引入微调BERT概率作为增强特征。

Result: XGBoost表现最佳，F1达到0.9747且总错误数最少；加入BERT概率特征后F1提升至0.9782，测试错误从241降至207。相比决策树基线（F1 0.9323），主要提升来自话语上下文特征和提升集成策略，BERT提供适度互补信号。

Conclusion: 该流水线在自我介绍检测任务上表现优异，BERT增强的XGBoost模型进一步提升了性能；对误报的分析表明部分误报实为源数据中姓名不一致导致的真实自我介绍，意味着实测指标实际上低估了真实性能。

Abstract: Self-introductions are common in legislative committee testimonies. Successfully detecting them and extracting the speaker's name is enormously helpful in the task of speaker identification in the context of government meetings. In this paper, we present a pipeline for detection of self-introductions in legislative committee testimony using machine learning. We construct a training dataset from 1.54 million utterances spanning five state legislative sessions, apply a name-matching heuristic to generate automatic labels, and train three classifiers: a decision tree, random forest, and XGBoost to find self-introductions and extract the speaker's name. We construct a feature set combining bag-of-words, positional context, structural signals, introductory phrase indicators, and discourse context features. Among the three classifiers, XGBoost achieves the best performance with an F1 score of 0.9747 and the fewest total errors; adding fine-tuned BERT probability features improves this further. As an extension, we score the full candidate dataset with a fine-tuned BERT classifier and add BERT probability outputs as features. This BERT-augmented XGBoost model improves F1 from 0.9747 to 0.9782 and reduces total test errors from 241 to 207. The primary gain over the decision tree baseline (F1 0.9323) is driven by discourse context features and the boosting ensemble strategy; BERT provides a modest complementary signal. Analysis of false positives reveals that a minority are genuine self-introductions mislabeled due to name inconsistencies in the source data, indicating that measured metrics modestly understate true performance.

</details>


### [279] [Thinking Hard, Not Smart: Reasoning Models Fail to Ration Test-Time Compute Across Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07968)
*Chenrui Fan,Yize Cheng,Ming Li,Yongyuan Liang,Tianyi Zhou,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个考试式评估框架，研究推理语言模型如何在多问题共享推理预算（token预算）的情况下进行全局分配。研究发现，无论开源还是前沿推理模型，都无法在不同难度和分值的问题间战略性分配共享预算，模型表现为贪婪的顺序求解器：按呈现顺序优先处理问题、在早期问题上过度投入、对分值不敏感，且这些倾向随问题数量增加而加剧。显式规划提示只能让计算分配更均匀，但无法产生基于难度或分值的优先级排序。这一行为模式从数学推理延伸到代码推理，表明全局预算分配是一个独立能力，传统逐问题评估无法捕捉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理模型评估通常一次只研究一个问题，但在现实应用中，多个问题共享端到端的成本或延迟约束，模型需要决定如何在问题之间划分有限的推理计算资源。本文旨在引入一个考试式评估框架来研究这种全局预算分配场景，填补现有评估方法的空白。

Method: 提出了一个考试式评估框架：模型需要在一个共享的token预算下，将预算分配给不同难度和分值的多个问题，以最大化总分。在多个开源和前沿推理模型上进行了系统实验，并通过规划提示（planning prompts）干预来测试模型的可控性，同时将行为模式从数学推理扩展到代码推理进行验证。

Result: 模型在全局预算分配上表现不佳：表现为贪婪顺序求解行为，按呈现顺序优先而非按难度或分值优先；对早期问题过度投入；对分值不敏感；这些倾向随问题数量增加而更显著。显式规划提示只能使计算分配更均匀，但无法实现价值或难度感知的优先级排序。同样的行为模式在数学和代码推理任务中一致出现。

Conclusion: 全局预算分配是当前推理模型尚不具备的一项独立能力，现有的逐问题评估无法捕捉这一能力缺陷。这项工作为推理模型在多问题场景下的资源分配研究建立了新的评估范式，并指出该方向仍是一个开放挑战。

Abstract: Reasoning language models increasingly use test-time compute to improve performance, but existing evaluations typically study this compute one question at a time. Yet when multiple problems share an end-to-end cost or latency constraint, models must decide how to divide limited inference compute among them. We introduce an exam-style evaluation framework for studying this setting, in which a model must distribute one shared token budget across questions with different difficulty and point values to maximize its total score. Across several open and frontier reasoning models, we find that models fail to allocate a shared budget strategically across questions of varying difficulties and values. Models behave largely as greedy sequential solvers: they prioritize questions by presentation order, front-load effort on early questions, and remain insensitive to value, with these tendencies becoming more pronounced as the number of questions grows. Explicit planning prompts spread compute more evenly but do not produce value- or difficulty-aware prioritization. The same behavioral pattern extends from mathematical to code reasoning. These findings establish global budget allocation as a distinct capability that is not captured by conventional per-question evaluation and remains a challenge for current reasoning models.

</details>


### [280] [Prompt Embedding Probes (PEP): Hallucination Detection in LLMs from Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08024)
*Zakhar Mrykhin,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出提示嵌入探针（PEP），一种用于大语言模型幻觉检测的白盒方法，通过在隐藏状态上扩展传统线性探针并加入少量可学习的提示嵌入，在TriviaQA、GSM8K和MedQA数据集上的多项评估中展示了优于标准线性探针的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然能生成流畅且有用的回答，但仍然容易产生幻觉（hallucination），现有方法在答案级别的幻觉检测上存在改进空间，需要更有效的白盒检测手段。

Method: PEP（Prompt Embedding Probes）在冻结的LLM的隐藏状态基础上扩展标准线性探针，通过向输入中注入少量可学习的提示嵌入（prompt embeddings）来增强检测能力，整个过程中骨干网络保持冻结，仅增加少量可训练参数。

Result: 在TriviaQA、GSM8K和MedQA数据集上使用多个规模的Qwen3模型进行评估，PEP在主要分布内设置中优于标准线性探针；在预生成预测和跨模型迁移场景中依然有效，但跨数据集迁移仍存在困难。

Conclusion: 基于提示的适配方法能够增强隐藏状态探针的幻觉检测能力，以少量可训练参数为代价保持骨干模型冻结，但跨数据集泛化仍是一个待解决的挑战。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent and useful responses but remain prone to hallucinations. We introduce Prompt Embedding Probes (PEP), a white-box method for answer-level hallucination detection from the hidden states of a frozen LLM. PEP extends standard linear probes by augmenting the input with a small number of learnable prompt embeddings. We evaluate PEP on TriviaQA, GSM8K, and MedQA using Qwen3 models at multiple scales. PEP improves hidden-state-based detection over standard linear probes in the main in-distribution setting. We further evaluate PEP for pre-generation prediction, cross-model transfer, and out-of-distribution generalization. PEP remains effective in the pre-generation and cross-model settings, whereas robust cross-dataset transfer remains difficult. These results show that prompt-based adaptation can strengthen hidden-state probing while keeping the backbone frozen and adding only a small number of trainable parameters.

</details>


### [281] [APEX-VW: A Document-Level English-Spanish Post-Editing Dataset in the Healthcare Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08059)
*Marie Escribe,Tharindu Ranasinghe,Amal Haddad Haddad,Hansi Hettiarachchi,Damith Premasiri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了APEX-VW语料库，一个基于英国NHS虚拟病房文档构建的开放英西（EN-ES）数据集，记录了专业译员在Trados Studio中对四种机器翻译系统输出进行人工后期编辑的过程，旨在研究术语规范化、修正传播和文档级别自动后期编辑（APE）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APE语料库大多在句子级别工作，或为合成数据，无法反映真实CAT工作流中术语修正如何在多个片段间传播的核心问题，缺乏支持文档级别研究的资源。

Method: 从NHS虚拟病房文档中选取7个文档连贯的源文本（共42k词），使用四种代表不同范式的MT系统翻译后，由专业译员在Trados Studio中进行后期编辑，并控制MT、术语和QA设置，构建保持文档顺序和CAT工具上下文的语料库。

Result: 构建了APEX-VW语料库，包含42k词的七个文档级源文本、四种MT系统的输出及专业人工后期编辑结果，是首个保留文档秩序和CAT工具上下文、适用于文档级APE研究的开放资源。

Conclusion: APEX-VW语料库作为一个新的基准资源，克服了现有语料库在句子级别和合成数据方面的局限，为文档级APE、修正传播感知辅助工具以及人工在环翻译支持研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Post-Editing (PE) of Machine Translation (MT) output often involves repeating the same lexical and terminological corrections across many segments, especially in specialised and highly repetitive documents. Despite substantial work on Automatic Post-Editing (APE), most available corpora operate at the sentence level, others are synthetic, and overall not designed to study how corrections propagate in realistic Computer-Assisted Translation (CAT) workflows. This paper presents the APEX-VW (Automatic Post-Editing eXperiments on Virtual Wards) Corpus, a new open English-Spanish (EN-ES) dataset built from recent NHS virtual-ward documents and professional PE in Trados Studio, with controlled MT, terminology, and quality assurance settings. The corpus contains seven document-coherent source texts totalling 42k words, translated with four MT systems representing different paradigms and then post-edited by professional translators. Unlike prior resources such as WMT APE corpora, eSCAPE, MLQE-PE, or LangMark, the dataset preserves document order and CAT-tool context, making it suitable for research on terminology normalisation, correction propagation, and human-in-the-loop translation support. The paper describes the corpus design, data preparation, PE setup, and initial corpus statistics, and positions the resource as a benchmark for document-level APE and propagation-aware assistive tools.

</details>


### [282] [DialectS2S: End-to-End Speech Dialogue Modeling for Low-Resource Chinese Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08067)
*Yi Shu,Tianyu Peng,Yingzhuo Deng,Wen Yang,Jun Lin,Changming Xie,Xinyu Yu,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DialectS2S，一种面向中文方言的端到端语音对话模型。通过可扩展的方言语音对话合成管道和两阶段后训练策略（结合自对齐语音监督），解决了低资源方言场景中数据稀缺及语义表示与语音监督不一致的问题，在多方言语音对话任务上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端语音对话模型主要面向主流语言，在低资源方言场景中受限于方言语音数据稀缺。此外，方言适应过程中模型的语义表示空间持续演化，而传统语音监督保持不变，导致隐藏表示与语音目标之间出现语义不一致，降低语音生成稳定性和自然度。

Method: （1）构建可扩展的方言语音对话合成管道，用于高效数据构造；（2）提出两阶段后训练策略，引入自对齐语音监督，使语音监督的语义内容与模型演化的语义表示对齐，从而提升方言语音生成质量。

Result: 在多个中文方言的语音对话任务中，DialectS2S一致性地优于现有基线，在方言一致性、回复质量和语音可理解性方面均取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: DialectS2S为低资源方言场景下的端到端语音对话建模提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案，并已全面开源模型检查点、训练数据集和微调代码，以促进后续研究与应用。

Abstract: Current end-to-end speech dialogue models are primarily optimized for mainstream languages and remain limited in low-resource dialect scenarios due to the scarcity of dialect speech data. Moreover, during dialect adaptation, the semantic representation space of speech dialogue models continuously evolves, while conventional speech supervision remains unchanged, leading to semantic inconsistency between hidden representations and speech targets and degrading speech stability and naturalness. To address these issues, we propose DialectS2S, an end-to-end speech dialogue model for Chinese dialects. We first develop a scalable dialect speech dialogue synthesis pipeline for efficient data construction. We further introduce a two-stage post-training strategy with self-aligned speech supervision, which aligns the semantic content of speech supervision with the evolved semantic representations of the model to improve dialect speech generation quality. Experimental results show that DialectS2S consistently outperforms existing baselines across multiple Chinese dialects in speech dialogue, achieving substantial improvements in dialect consistency, response quality, and speech intelligibility. Our work provides an efficient and scalable solution for end-to-end speech dialogue modeling in low-resource dialect scenarios. To facilitate future research and practical applications, we fully open-source the DialectS2S framework, including model checkpoints, training datasets, and fine-tuning code.

</details>


### [283] [Commitment Before Realization: When Classifier-Free Guidance Becomes Unnecessary in Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08082)
*Fan Zhou,Weitian Wang,Tim Van de Cruys*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了掩码扩散语言模型中分类器自由引导（CFG）何时真正必要的问题。通过比较从任意部分输出出发、在继续CFG与仅使用基础模型两种路径下的最终约束满足概率，定义了引导的"剩余价值"和承诺视界（commitment horizon）。结果显示，许多提示无需CFG即可成功，即使需要引导，其收益也通常集中在早期阶段。在各自的交叉拟合视界处冻结解码，在全部13个子任务上均不劣于全程CFG，同时还能通过重新开放承诺位置来改善失败轨迹的恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFG在掩码扩散语言模型解码过程中通常全程保持开启，但不同提示和时间点上其收益差异很大。作者希望系统性地研究CFG到底在何时是真正需要的，以避免不必要的计算开销和潜在的负面影响。

Method: 从任意部分输出出发，比较继续CFG与仅用基础模型继续解码两种策略下最终约束满足的概率差异，定义该差异为引导的剩余价值。界定承诺视界a*为最早可以从该点起切换到基础模型而最终成功率下降不超过设定容差的时间点。利用基础模型下对应的成功率（committor）是鞅的性质，分析了CFG每步效果与引导对数方向和后继committor协方差的关系，并通过交叉拟合确定每个提示的个性化视界。

Result: 引导依赖性高度依赖于具体提示：许多提示无需CFG即可成功，部分提示中CFG无收益甚至有害。有收益的提示中，收益往往集中在早期。在不同提示中，承诺视界通常在早期出现，且在同一约束家族内的变异大于不同家族之间的差异。在交叉拟合的视界处冻结每个提示，在全部13个子任务的事先设定容差下不劣于全程CFG，即使此时仍有许多token被掩码。在承诺区域之后，提高并行度仅对约束成功率造成很小代价，但流利度仍会因并行宽度而下降。对于失败的轨迹，重新开放已承诺的位置在两种失败模式下都能改善恢复。

Conclusion: 该研究将"承诺"（commitment）与"实现"（realization）分离，表明CFG并非全程必需。个性化的早期切换策略可以在不牺牲约束满足率的前提下节省大量计算，同时重新开放机制为失败轨迹提供了额外的修复途径。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) is usually kept on throughout masked diffusion language model decoding, although its benefit varies across prompts and over time. We study when CFG is actually needed by comparing, from any partial output, the probability of eventual constraint satisfaction under continued CFG and under base-only continuation. Their difference defines the remaining value of guidance. Guidance dependence is highly prompt-specific. Many prompts already succeed without CFG, while for others it provides no measurable benefit or can be harmful. For prompts that do benefit, the gain is often concentrated early. We define the commitment horizon $\astar$ as the earliest point from which switching all remaining decoding to the base model reduces final success by no more than a chosen tolerance. Under the base model, the corresponding success probability, or committor, is a martingale. To first order, CFG's per-step effect is governed by the covariance between the guidance logit direction and the successor committor. This gives a local account of when guidance can help, but it does not by itself locate the horizon. Among prompts with an observed preterminal horizon, $\astar$ is usually early and varies more within constraint families than between them. Freezing each prompt at its own cross-fitted horizon is noninferior to full CFG on all 13 subtasks at the prespecified margin, even while many tokens remain masked. This separates commitment from realization. The boundary also identifies a later region in which higher parallelism adds only a small cost in constraint success, although fluency still degrades with parallel width. For failed trajectories, reopening committed positions improves recovery in both failure modes.

</details>


### [284] [Archer: Adaptive Reuse of Cached Hidden States for Efficient Rollback in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08086)
*Xuning He,Zinan Sheng,Yongding Tao,Huanyu Liu,Ge Li,Xue Jiang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Archer，一种无需训练的KV缓存方法，用于支持回滚的扩散语言模型（DLM）。Archer通过不对称地复用提示词的K/V状态、同时保持响应状态与当前假设同步，在保持或提升生成质量的同时实现2.57倍平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）具备回滚能力，可以随上下文演化修正早期预测，但每次去噪更新都会改变全局上下文，导致提示词和响应状态都被重复计算，推理成本高昂。传统的KV缓存假设历史状态不可变，难以与回滚机制兼容，因此需要一种能兼顾回滚能力和推理效率的缓存策略。

Method: Archer采用非对称缓存策略：在有界状态邻域内复用提示词的K/V缓存，同时保持可变的响应状态与当前假设同步。通过有界复用摊销重复的提示词计算，避免缓存可变的响应状态；同时延迟来自暂定token的反馈，减少对瞬时高置信度错误的过早强化，为回滚提供更多纠错机会。论文还提供了理论分析，将提示词复用刻画为与可逆性对齐的缓存边界，并给出了保持全刷新决策的decoder-margin条件。

Result: 在主测试套件上，Archer达到33.63%的最佳平均性能，同时获得2.57倍平均加速；在多种评估设置下，Pass@1最高提升3.05个百分点，加速比最高达2.95倍。

Conclusion: Archer在DLM加速中打破了"质量换速度"的权衡，通过有界状态感知的KV复用策略同时实现了性能提升和推理加速，为回滚型扩散语言模型的高效推理提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) iteratively refine a sequence, allowing earlier predictions to be revised as context evolves. This rollback capability distinguishes them from irreversible autoregressive generation, but makes inference costly. Every denoising update alters the global context, forcing both prompt and response states to be recomputed even though only response tokens are revisable. Key-value (KV) caching could reduce this cost, yet conventional caching assumes immutable historical states and is therefore difficult to reconcile with rollback.In this paper, we introduce Adaptive Reuse of Cached Hidden States for Efficient Rollback (Archer), a training-free KV caching method for rollback-capable DLMs. Archer asymmetrically keeps the mutable response synchronized with the current hypothesis while reusing prompt K/V within a bounded state neighborhood. Although prompt representations also change under bidirectional attention, their token identities remain fixed; bounded reuse therefore amortizes repeated prompt computation without caching mutable response states. It also delays feedback from tentative tokens, reducing premature reinforcement of transient high-confidence errors and giving rollback more opportunity to correct them. Our analysis characterizes prompt reuse as a reversibility-aligned cache boundary, bounds its state-dependent approximation error, and gives a decoder-margin condition for preserving full-refresh decisions.Existing DLM acceleration often trades quality for speed. Archer shifts this frontier, attaining the best mean performance of 33.63% together with a 2.57x mean speedup on the main suite. Across evaluated settings, it improves Pass@1 by up to 3.05 points and reaches up to 2.95x speedup. Controlled analyses connect the quality gain to delayed prompt feedback and validate state-aware refresh. Our code is available at https://github.com/Hxnng/Archer.

</details>


### [285] [Wisdom in Unity: The Role of Multilingual Training in Figurative Language Identification in Proverbs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08090)
*Rama Alomair,Remas Alsubaie,Walaa Saifalislam,Rima Alsonbul,Mona Alnajjar,Razan Aldossari,Haya Alibrahim,Abeer Aldayel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言监督在谚语比喻识别中的作用，使用742个谚语概念、6,787个翻译实例和七种语言，提出四维标注框架（隐喻、道德/劝诫、因果、文化特定），发现约50%的翻译数据即可达到近最优性能，且文化特定形式在多语言监督下获益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言比喻识别虽非新课题，但超越同质语言训练数据需要更清晰地理解翻译后的多语言监督贡献，现有研究缺乏对此的系统性量化分析。

Method: 使用742个谚语概念、6,787个翻译实例、七种语言构建数据集；引入四维标注框架（Metaphorical、Moral/Advisory、Cause-Effect、Culture Specific）对谚语进行多维表征；评估五种模型（包括多语言编码器和指令微调LLM），在逐步增加的多语言监督级别下进行对比实验。

Result: 约50%的翻译多语言训练数据即可达到近最优的比喻识别性能；结合多样化比喻形式可获得最强整体性能；文化特定形式虽最不常见但在多语言监督下性能增益最大；道德/劝诫和文化特定形式对指令微调LLM的比喻识别贡献最大。

Conclusion: 多语言比喻识别应超越以隐喻为中心的单一分类法，转向概念级的多维框架，显式建模互补的比喻意义形式，以更全面有效地提升跨语言比喻理解能力。

Abstract: Although multilingual approaches to figurative language identification are not new, the shift beyond language homogeneous training data requires a clearer understanding of the contribution of translated multilingual supervision. We examine this question using 742 proverb concepts across 6,787 translated instances in seven languages. We evaluate five models, including multilingual encoders and instruction tuned LLMs, under progressively increasing levels of multilingual supervision. Moreover, we introduce a multidimensional annotation framework for proverbs that characterizes them through four complementary figurative forms: Metaphorical, Moral/Advisory, Cause-Effect, and Culture Specific.
  Our findings show that approximately 50% of the translated multilingual training data is sufficient to achieve near-optimal figurative language identification performance. We further show that combining diverse figurative forms yields the strongest overall performance. A notable finding is that the least frequent figurative form, Culture Specific, exhibits the largest performance gains under multilingual supervision. Furthermore, the Moral/Advisory and Culture Specific forms contribute most to the performance of instruction-tuned LLMs on figurative language identification. These findings motivate multilingual figurative language identification to move beyond metaphor-centric taxonomies toward concept level multidimensional frameworks that explicitly model complementary forms of figurative meaning.

</details>


### [286] [NeuPAT: Neuron-aware Plasticity Allocation Tuning for Language-Preserving MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08107)
*Jiayue Jin,Jingwei Zhang,Chen Wang,Jing Liu,Longteng Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NeuPAT是一个轻量级且架构无关的框架，通过估计神经元适应性模式，在多模态指令微调中分配神经元级更新约束，保护语言敏感神经元同时促进多模态适应，恢复了94.5%的语言能力退化且保持相当的多模态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态扩展LLM时往往会损害预训练获得的语言智能，需要理解并解决这种能力退化问题。

Method: 提出NeuPAT框架，包含小型探测阶段以估计神经元适应模式，并基于此选择性地保护语言敏感神经元，同时通过更可塑的神经元促进多模态适应，在微调过程中分配神经元级更新约束。

Result: 在多种LLM家族实验中，NeuPAT在11个语言基准上恢复了普通微调导致的94.5%语言能力退化，同时保持了相当的多模态性能。

Conclusion: 通过神经元级可塑性分配，可以在多模态扩展时有效保留语言能力，为能力保持的多模态扩展提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Multimodal expansion of large language models (LLMs) enables new perceptual capabilities but often compromises the language intelligence acquired during pretraining. In this work, we investigate this phenomenon from the perspective of internal adaptation dynamics and discover that neurons in pretrained LLMs exhibit heterogeneous plasticity during multimodal learning: some neurons are critical for preserving language capabilities, while others are more adaptive to multimodal knowledge. Based on this insight, we propose NeuPAT (Neuron-aware Plasticity Allocation Tuning), a lightweight and architecture-agnostic framework that allocates neuron-wise update constraints during multimodal instruction tuning. NeuPAT uses a small-scale probing stage to estimate neuron adaptation patterns and selectively protects language-sensitive neurons while promoting multimodal adaptation through more plastic neurons. Experiments across diverse LLM families demonstrate that NeuPAT recovers 94.5\% of the language capability degradation caused by vanilla tuning on 11 language benchmarks while maintaining comparable multimodal performance, providing an effective approach for capability-preserving multimodal expansion.

</details>
